### [102] [Compact stars in a large-tension braneworld: mildly negative Weyl coupling consistent with NICER and gravitational-wave data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09836)
*Samuel Isidoro dos Santos Júnior,Rafael Camargo Rodrigues de Lima,Pedro Henrique Ribeiro da Silva Moraes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用贝叶斯推理对多信使观测数据进行分析，在Sahni–Shtanov膜世界模型中约束致密星的参数空间。基于SLy物态方程，通过MCMC分析结合GW170817和NICER观测数据，得到膜张力较大、Weyl耦合轻度负值的结果，最大质量M_max=2.30^+0.14_-0.08 M⊙，R_1.4=13.31^+0.54_-0.57 km，高于GR预言，且与当前观测一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在膜世界引力框架下，利用多信使天文观测（引力波+X射线）约束致密星（中子星/夸克星）的物态和引力修正参数，检验超出广义相对论的新物理。

Method: 采用有效Sahni–Shtanov膜世界模型，引入修正的Tolman–Oppenheimer–Volkoff方程（包含局域二次膜修正和非局域Weyl扇区的参数化闭合）。利用SLy物态方程，结合GW170817（LIGO/Virgo）和NICER对PSR J0740+6620、PSR J1231−1411的观测质量-半径后验，进行仿射不变系综MCMC分析。

Result: 膜张力log10(λ/km^−2)=3.98^+1.52_-1.44（68%），处于大张力区域；Weyl耦合α_𝒰=−0.15^+0.30_-0.08（68%）；w_𝒰弱约束，95%置信区间[−1.25, 1.02]。最大质量M_max=2.30^+0.14_-0.08 M⊙，R_1.4=13.31^+0.54_-0.57 km（68%），均高于GR预言。95%后验区间延伸至GW190814次级质量范围。

Conclusion: 大张力膜世界模型结合轻度负Weyl耦合与当前NICER和引力波观测一致，无需对广义相对论进行大幅修正。

Abstract: We use Bayesian inference on multi-messenger observations to constrain the parameter space of compact stars in a phenomenological braneworld model inspired by the effective Sahni--Shtanov scenario. Stellar structure is described by modified Tolman--Oppenheimer--Volkoff equations including local quadratic brane corrections and a phenomenological closure for the nonlocal Weyl sector, parametrized by the brane tension $λ$, the Weyl coupling $α_{\mathcal{U}}$, and the Weyl equation-of-state parameter $w_{\mathcal{U}}$. Using the SLy equation of state, we perform an affine-invariant ensemble Markov Chain Monte Carlo analysis combining mass--radius posteriors from GW170817 (LIGO/Virgo) and NICER observations of PSR~J0740$+$6620 and PSR~J1231$-$1411. The posterior yields $\log_{10}(λ/\mathrm{km}^{-2})=3.98^{+1.52}{-1.44}$ (68%), indicating a large-brane-tension regime where local high-energy corrections are subdominant. The Weyl coupling is constrained to $α{\mathcal{U}}=-0.15^{+0.30}{-0.08}$ (68%), while $w{\mathcal{U}}$ remains weakly constrained, with a 95% credible interval of $[-1.25,,1.02]$. The inferred stellar properties are $M_{\max}=2.30^{+0.14}{-0.08},M\odot$ and $R_{1.4}=13.31^{+0.54}_{-0.57},\mathrm{km}$ (68%), exceeding the corresponding General Relativity predictions for the SLy equation of state. The 95% posterior interval extends into the GW190814 secondary-mass range, although the median and best-fit values remain below it. These results show that a large-tension braneworld with a mildly negative Weyl coupling is consistent with current NICER and gravitational-wave constraints without requiring large deviations from General Relativity.

</details>


### [103] [Black-bounce spacetime and galactic rotation curves: from singularity resolution to observable gravitational effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09840)
*Farook Rahaman Aritra Sanyal Saibal Ray*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了黑跳时空度规（一种通过参数a消除中心奇点的Schwarzschild修正度规）在三类观测窗口中的物理效应：黑洞视界结构与可穿越虫洞的转变、太阳系精密检验（水星近日点进动、引力透镜、Shapiro时延），以及首次应用于星系动力学——拟合NGC 7331旋转曲线，得到a=1.035±0.119 kpc的观测约束，证实该度规能在与标准暗物质晕一致的前提下正则化星系内核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Schwarzschild黑洞存在中心奇点这一理论缺陷，黑跳时空通过引入一个自由参数a消除了奇点，但其物理可行性尚未经过多尺度观测检验。本文旨在系统评估该度规在太阳系和星系尺度上的观测兼容性。

Method: 1) 解析分析黑跳度规的视界结构及a<2M（规则黑洞）与a>2M（可穿越虫洞）之间的相变条件；2) 计算太阳系精密测试中的后牛顿修正（水星近日点进动、引力透镜偏折角、Shapiro时延），与GR对比；3) 首次推导黑跳度规在星系尺度上的圆速度场，对NGC 7331旋转曲线进行四组分质量分解（黑跳中心势+Freeman指数盘+Hernquist核球+NFW暗物质晕），拟合得到a值。

Result: 1) 明确了a=2M为规则黑洞与可穿越虫洞的分界点；2) 太阳系检验表明参数a引入小但可测量的GR修正；3) 对NGC 7331的最优拟合得到a=1.035±0.119 kpc，首次从星系动力学给出弹跳参数a的观测约束，且该度规在大半径处与标准NFW暗晕完全一致。

Conclusion: 黑跳时空在正则化内核奇点的同时与标准暗物质晕在大尺度上兼容，能够同时通过太阳系精密检验和星系旋转曲线拟合，为无奇点黑洞替代方案提供了一个有说服力的理论试验场。

Abstract: The black-bounce spacetime provides a regularized modification of the Schwarzschild metric by introducing a free parameter $a$ that eliminates the central singularity while preserving the exterior geometry of a classical black hole. This work investigates the physical implications of this metric across three complementary observational windows. First, we examine the horizon structure and the transition between a regular black hole ($a < 2M$) and a traversable wormhole ($a > 2M$), offering a viable singularity-free alternative to classical black hole solutions. Second, we perform Solar System precision tests perihelion precession of Mercury, gravitational lensing, and the Shapiro time delay of radar signals demonstrating that the bounce parameter $a$ introduces small but measurable corrections relative to general relativity. Third, and for the first time, we apply the black-bounce metric to galactic dynamics by deriving the circular velocity field and fitting it to the observed rotation curve of NGC~7331. A four-component mass decomposition black-bounce central potential, Freeman exponential disc, Hernquist bulge, and a Navarro--Frenk--White dark matter halo yields $a = 1.035 \pm 0.119$~kpc , providing the first observational constraint on the bounce parameter from galaxy kinematics. Our results confirm that the black-bounce metric regularises the inner galactic core while remaining fully consistent with a standard dark matter halo at large radii, establishing this spacetime as a compelling testbed for singularity-free alternatives to classical black holes.

</details>


### [104] [Perturbative aspects of analogue FLRW spacetime Jellium models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09843)
*Matheus E. Pereira,Hermano Velten,Francisco B. Lustosa,Alexandre G. M. Schmidt*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在均匀、各向同性且膨胀的Jellium模型（FLRW模型）中的电声扰动模式，模拟牛顿宇宙学膨胀中的密度扰动。提出了线性扰动的解析解和完整数值评估，并通过引入尺度依赖的粒子数涨落功率谱，表征了线性与非线性区间的扰动时域演化，为理论-实验对接提供工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过电声扰动模式研究类比牛顿宇宙学膨胀中的密度扰动，建立连接理论与实验的桥梁。

Method: 在FLRW Jellium模型中，分别采用解析求解线性扰动和全数值模拟方法，分析电声模式在压力支撑演化与非绝热贡献源下的时间演化，并引入尺度依赖的粒子数涨落功率谱。

Result: 获得了特定类比宇宙学膨胀中线性扰动的新解析解，以及完整数值评估结果，表征了电声模式在线性与非线性区间的动力学行为，并展示了粒子数涨落功率谱对物理参数的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 所提出的粒子数涨落功率谱可作为连接理论与实验的有效工具，其依赖于模型物理参数的特性为实验验证提供了方向。

Abstract: We study electro-acoustic perturbative modes in homogeneous, isotropic and expanding -- dubbed as Friedmann-Lemaitre-Robertson-Walker (FLRW) -- Jellium models, thereby mimicking density perturbations in analogous Newtonian cosmological expansions. We present both novel analytic solutions for linear perturbations in specific analogue cosmological expansions and full numerical evaluations that characterize the temporal evolution of the electro-acoustic modes capturing their full dynamical behavior across the linear and the nonlinear regimes. For both the case of pressure supported evolution or modes sourced by nonadiabatic contributions, we also characterize the temporal evolution of such perturbations by introducing their scale dependent particle number fluctuation power spectrum which can act as a tool to connect theory and experiments. The dependence of the latter on the physical parameters of the model is demonstrated in detail.

</details>


### [105] [GRACE: An Open-Source Framework for GPU-Accelerated Numerical Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09854)
*Carlo Musolino,Christian Ecker,Konrad Topolski,Marie Cassing,Keneth Miler,Harry Ho-Yin Ng,Khalil Pierre,Elias R. Most,Luciano Rezzolla*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: GRACE是一个基于Kokkos和p4est开源库的全新GPU加速数值相对论框架，支持CPU/GPU异构计算，实现了理想GRMHD与爱因斯坦方程的耦合求解，通过多标准测试验证，并在双中子星合并模拟中与FIL代码取得一致结果，同时发布了后处理工具GRACEpy。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一个高效运行于异构高性能计算平台、完全基于开源库构建、支持GPU加速的数值相对论框架，以满足现代天体物理模拟对计算效率和可移植性的需求。

Method: 采用Kokkos实现CPU/GPU架构的性能可移植性，使用p4est进行自适应网格细化；在固定或自适应网格上自洽求解Z4c公式的爱因斯坦方程与理想GRMHD方程，并通过约束传输维持无散磁场。

Result: 通过从磁化激波管、磁转子到磁化Bondi吸积、黑洞铃宕、中子星振荡谱、双黑洞合并等一系列标准测试验证；在双中子星合并（等质量无磁化与不等质量磁化）中与FIL代码吻合；报告了单设备吞吐量及多GPU/CPU架构上的强弱扩展性结果。

Conclusion: GRACE是一个经过充分验证、高性能、完全开源的数值相对论框架，具备良好的可移植性和扩展性，并配套发布了后处理工具GRACEpy。

Abstract: We present GRACE, a new GPU-accelerated numerical-relativity framework designed to run efficiently on heterogeneous high-performance computing platforms. Developed from scratch and built exclusively on open-source libraries, GRACE employs Kokkos for performance portability across CPU and GPU architectures and p4est for adaptive mesh refinement. The code evolves the equations of ideal GRMHD -- with divergence-free magnetic fields maintained by constrained transport -- self-consistently coupled to the Einstein equations in the Z4c formulation, on fixed or adaptively refined grids. We validate the implementation against a suite of standard tests, ranging from magnetized shock tubes and the magnetic rotor in flat spacetime, through (magnetized) Bondi accretion onto a Schwarzschild black hole and the ringdown of a perturbed spinning puncture, to neutron-star oscillation spectra in fixed and dynamical spacetimes and the merger of binary black holes. As more demanding applications, we evolve two binary neutron-star mergers -- an equal-mass, unmagnetized system with an ideal-gas equation of state and an unequal-mass, magnetized system with a finite-temperature tabulated equation of state -- finding the inspiral dynamics to agree well with the FIL code. We also report single-device throughput together with strong- and weak-scaling results on multiple GPU and CPU architectures. GRACE is publicly released together with GRACEpy, a basic post-processing and data-analysis environment.

</details>


### [106] [Hawking Radiation in $f(\mathcal{R})$ Gravity: Survival of lighter black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09863)
*Mriganka Dutta,Panchajanya Dey,Banibrata Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究f(R)修改引力理论中的霍金辐射，发现原始黑洞在该理论下蒸发更慢，质量阈值（~5×10¹³克）比广义相对论低至少一个数量级，从而放宽了原始黑洞作为暗物质候选者的约束条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论在描述引力现象上非常成功，但现代宇宙学和天体物理学中的若干开放问题（如暴胀、暗能量）暗示需要扩展或修改该框架。修改引力理论试图填补这些空白，同时保持与实验测试的一致性。

Method: 在f(R)引力理论框架下研究霍金辐射，其中R是标量曲率，并分析其对作为暗物质候选者的原始黑洞的影响。

Result: 与广义相对论预测相比，原始黑洞在修改引力中蒸发更慢。非旋转黑洞若质量≤5×10¹³克（取决于修改引力参数），可能存活至今，该质量阈值比广义相对论低至少十倍。

Conclusion: 推迟的蒸发时间线放宽了原始黑洞作为暗物质成分的可行性约束，重塑了暗物质问题可能解决方案的格局，并激励进一步研究替代引力理论及其宇宙学后果。

Abstract: Einstein's theory of general relativity (GR) has been remarkably successful in describing gravitational phenomena. However, several open questions in modern cosmology and astrophysics (e.g. inflation, dark energy) suggest the need for extensions or modifications to this framework. Modified gravity (MGR) theories, including scalar-tensor models and higher-dimensional approaches, attempt to address these gaps while maintaining consistency with established experimental tests. This work investigates Hawking radiation within an $f(\mathcal{R})$ gravity theory, with $\mathcal{R}$ being scalar curvature, focusing on its implications for primordial black holes (PBHs) as potential dark matter (DM) candidates. Our analysis reveals that PBHs evaporate slowly in MGR compared to GR predictions. Specifically, we find that non-rotating black holes with masses $\sim 5 \times 10^{13}$ g or lower would have survived by the present epoch, depending on the MGR parameter-a mass threshold approximately at least ten times smaller than in GR. This retarded evaporation timeline imposes relaxed new constraints on the viability of PBHs as DM constituents, thereby reshaping the landscape of possible solutions to the DM problem. This motivates further investigation into alternative gravitational theories and their cosmological consequences.

</details>


### [107] [Exceptional lines in the Kerr-Newman black hole spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09878)
*João Paulo Cavalcante,Maurício Richartz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究了Kerr-Newman黑洞在近极端区域中的大规模标量扰动，重点分析了(ℓ,m)=(1,1)拟正规模式谱，发现了异常线序列（频率简并）和相应的异常点，以及这些退化与零阻尼和阻尼模式分支之间的密切关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Kerr-Newman黑洞在近极端条件下的拟正规模式频谱特性，特别是大规模标量扰动下的异常点和几何相位行为。

Method: 通过分析(ℓ,m)=(1,1)拟正规模式谱，识别异常线和异常点序列；利用参数空间中闭合环路输运势谱，分析异常点相关的几何相位；推导极端极限下零阻尼模式的频率解析表达式。

Result: 发现了近极端Kerr-Newman黑洞中拟正规模式的异常线序列，证实了无质量场对应的异常点存在；揭示了谱退化为零阻尼和阻尼模式的分支结构。

Conclusion: Kerr-Newman黑洞在近极端区域存在丰富的拟正规模式退化结构，异常点和几何相位现象与谱的分支化密切相关，零阻尼模式的解析表达式得以推导。

Abstract: We investigate massive scalar perturbations of Kerr-Newman black holes, focusing on the $(\ell,m) = (1,1)$ quasinormal mode spectrum in the near-extremal regime. We identify a sequence of exceptional lines, at which overtone frequencies become degenerate, together with a corresponding sequence of exceptional points for massless fields. We analyze the geometric phases associated with these exceptional points by transporting the spectrum around closed loops in parameter space and examining the resulting permutation of quasinormal mode frequencies. We further show that these degeneracies are closely related to the branching of the spectrum into zero-damping and damped modes, and derive an analytic expression for the frequencies of the zero-damping modes in the extremal limit.

</details>


### [108] [Entropy and Non-Collapse in Lorentzian Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09940)
*Rohit Dhormare*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文建立了洛伦兹Raychaudhuri方程与Ricci流中Perelman非坍塌定理之间的几何对应关系，通过将Raychaudhuri方程解释为Ricci流的洛伦兹类比，将广义相对论中的测地线聚焦与几何分析中的单调性和熵泛函联系起来，推导出洛伦兹非坍塌定理，引入协变熵泛函，并提出测地线熵容量的概念，为引力、热力学和信息提供了一个统一的几何框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立广义相对论中Raychaudhuri方程与Ricci流中Perelman非坍塌定理之间的深层几何对应关系，从而在引力、热力学与信息理论之间构建统一的几何框架。

Method: 将Raychaudhuri方程解释为Ricci流的洛伦兹类比，利用这一对应关系将测地线聚焦与Perelman的单调性及熵泛函相联系，推导洛伦兹版本的非坍塌定理，并引入协变熵泛函来刻画因果体积的演化。

Result: 推导出洛伦兹非坍塌定理，定义了协变熵泛函，并提出了测地线熵容量的概念——一个曲率有界的信息存储上限。

Conclusion: 该工作为引力、热力学和信息理论提供了一个统一的几何框架，表明时空区域的曲率限制了其可能存储的信息量。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish a geometric correspondence between the Lorentzian Raychaudhuri equation and Perelman's non-collapsing theorem for the Ricci flow. By interpreting the Raychaudhuri equation as a Lorentzian analogue of Ricci flow, we connect geodesic focusing in general relativity to the monotonicity and entropy functionals of geometric analysis. Using this correspondence, we derive a Lorentzian non-collapsing theorem and introduce a covariant entropy functional governing causal volume evolution. Finally, we propose the concept of geodesic entropy capacity, a curvature-bounded limit on the information that can be stored in spacetime regions, providing a unified geometric framework linking gravitation, thermodynamics, and information.

</details>


### [109] [Scalar and vector bosons in a Bonnor-Melvin-$Λ$ spacetime: an exact Duffin-Kemmer-Petiau analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09997)
*Francisco A. Cruz Neto,Luis B. Castro*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在Bonnor-Melvin-Λ时空中，利用DKP形式论和Umezawa投影算子，严格求解了标量和矢量玻色子的动力学问题。径向方程化为含Pöschl–Teller势的类Schrödinger方程，通过Friedrichs自伴延拓将问题表述为奇异Sturm–Liouville问题，得到纯离散径向谱和闭式本征函数，统一且精确地处理了标量和矢量玻色子，明确了背景全局几何结构对禁闭和谱性质的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在弯曲时空背景下统一且精确地处理标量和矢量玻色子的动力学，超越此前基于锥形近似的分析方法，澄清背景全局几何结构（尤其是度规函数在离散点处为零所导致的奇异边界）对粒子禁闭和谱性质的完整影响。

Method: 采用DKF形式论描述弯曲时空中的自旋0和自旋1玻色子；利用Umezawa投影算子分离物理的自旋-0和自旋-1扇区；在完整弯曲时空中导出精确的二阶运动方程（不依赖锥形近似）；将径向方程化为含三角函数型Pöschl–Teller势的类Schrödinger方程；针对度规函数在离散径向点为零导致的奇异性，采用Friedrichs自伴延拓构造奇异Sturm–Liouville问题。

Result: 标量扇区径向方程化为含三角函数型Pöschl–Teller有效势的类Schrödinger方程；矢量扇区纵向模受相同有效势支配，横向极化由广义三角函数型Pöschl–Teller势描述；所有物理扇区均呈现纯离散径向谱；本征函数以闭式（closed form）获得。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了Bonnor-Melvin-Λ时空中标量和矢量玻色子统一的精确解，补充了此前基于锥形近似的分析，并阐明了背景全局几何结构在塑造禁闭和谱性质中的核心作用。

Abstract: We study scalar and vector bosons in the Bonnor--Melvin--$Λ$ spacetime within the Duffin--Kemmer--Petiau (DKP) formalism. By employing Umezawa's projection operators, we separate the physical spin-0 and spin-1 sectors and derive the corresponding exact second-order equations in the full curved spacetime, without relying on the conical approximation. For the scalar sector, the radial equation reduces to a Schrödinger-like equation with a trigonometric Pöschl--Teller effective potential. In the vector sector, the longitudinal mode is governed by the same effective potential, whereas the transverse polarizations are described by generalized trigonometric Pöschl--Teller potentials. Because the me\-tric function vanishes at a discrete set of radial points, the radial dynamics is naturally formulated as a singular Sturm--Liouville problem on a fundamental interval, with the physical radial domain fixed by the Friedrichs self-adjoint extension of the corresponding singular radial operators. As a result, all physical sectors exhibit purely discrete radial spectra, and their eigenfunctions are obtained in closed form. These results provide a unified exact treatment of scalar and vector bosons in the Bonnor--Melvin--$Λ$ spacetime, complement previous analyses based on the conical approximation, and clarify the role of the global geometric structure of the background in shaping confinement and spectral properties.

</details>


### [110] [Quasinormal modes of Bonanno-Reuter black holes via the Spectral Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10199)
*Davide Batic,Denys Dutykh,Fabio Scardigli*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用谱方法计算了Bonanno-Reuter正则黑洞（由渐近安全引力启发的修正Schwarzschild黑洞）在标量、电磁和引力扰动下的准正则模频谱，首次揭示了该黑洞存在纯虚数的过阻尼模以及大量高阶泛音，并指出这些特征在之前的高阶WKB分析中被完全遗漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索渐近安全引力框架下Bonanno-Reuter正则黑洞的准正则模特性，特别是验证谱方法相比传统WKB方法在揭示量子引力修正黑洞微扰谱方面的优越性，并填补先前研究中遗漏的过阻尼模和高阶泛音信息。

Method: 采用谱方法（Spectral Method）对标量、电磁和引力三类扰动分别计算QNM频谱，与高阶WKB方法进行精度对比；背景度规为引入重整化群修正后的Bonanno-Reuter Schwarzschild度规（含Anti-de Sitter核心，运行参数α>0）。

Result: 1) 谱方法相比高阶WKB方法具有更高精度；2) 成功识别了基模和大规模的高阶泛音；3) 首次发现纯虚数的过阻尼（overdamped）模，这些模式在之前的WKB分析中被完全遗漏。

Conclusion: 高精度谱方法对研究量子引力启发黑洞模型的微妙特征至关重要，Bonanno-Reuter黑洞存在此前未被发现的纯虚数过阻尼模，表明其微扰谱比预期更丰富。

Abstract: In this work, we explore the quasinormal modes (QNMs) of the Bonanno-Reuter black hole, one of the first regular black hole metric suggested by the Asymptotically Safe Gravity (ASG) program. The running parameter $α$ is set to a positive value, the related running Newton coupling vanishes at high energies, fully achieving an ultraviolet fixed point and eliminating non-physical UV divergences. This yields a singularity-free geometry. Hence, we focus on the resulting renormalisation-group-improved Schwarzschild metric, which naturally produces an (Anti)deSitter non-singular core. On the basis of this background, we compute the QNM spectrum for scalar, electromagnetic, and gravitational perturbations by employing the Spectral Method (SM). This method, recognised for its enhanced precision compared to high-order WKB methods, allows the identification of fundamental modes, extensive collections of overtones, and purely imaginary overdamped modes that were entirely missed in previous analyses. These characteristics, resolved here for the first time in the Bonanno-Reuter black hole, underscore the crucial importance of high-precision spectral methods in investigating delicate signatures of black hole models inspired by quantum gravity.

</details>


### [111] [Magnetising de Sitter and Anti-de Sitter spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10213)
*Yun-Ten Chin,Yen-Kheng Lim*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在球对称时空中对de Sitter (dS) 和 Anti-de Sitter (AdS) 时空进行磁化的方法。弱场情形下在固定dS/AdS背景中求解麦克斯韦方程得到磁场；强场情形下考虑爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦引力与引力流体源，通过Harrison变换得到dS/AdS型的Melvin磁宇宙解，并研究其物理与几何性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索dS和AdS时空在磁化作用下的行为，特别是强磁场对时空几何的反作用，填补dS/AdS背景下磁化时空研究的空白。

Method: 弱场方法：在固定dS/AdS背景中求解麦克斯韦方程。强场方法：考虑爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦引力与流体源，通过Harrison变换及流体能量-压力变换得到自洽的解。

Result: 得到了dS/AdS型的Melvin磁宇宙解，该解描述了强磁场对dS/AdS时空的变形效应。

Conclusion: 成功构建了dS和AdS背景下自洽的磁化时空解，并分析了其物理与几何特性。

Abstract: We attempt to magnetise the de Sitter (dS) and Anti-de Sitter (AdS) spacetimes in spherical symmetry. First, the weak field case is considered where Maxwell's equation is solved to find magnetic fields in fixed dS/AdS backgrounds. For strong magnetic fields we consider Einstein-Maxwell gravity with a gravitating fluid source. With gravitational backreaction of the magnetic field taken into account, the strong fields deform the dS/AdS spacetime, resulting in a dS/AdS-type analogue of the Melvin magnetic universe. This solution is obtained via a Harrison-like transformation, along with appropriate transformations of the fluid energy and pressures. Some of its physical and geometrical properties of the solution are studied.

</details>


### [112] [Probing the Strong Equivalence Principle through the External Field Effect. How Do Two Masses Fall?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10247)
*Ankit Kumar,Kelvin Tang Tee Tniam,Peng Chengxiaohe,Ng Li Yang,P. Arumugam,Tom Złośnik,Yen-Kheng Lim,Tomasz Paterek*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了修改引力理论（特别是MOND）所预言的外部场效应（External Field Effect）对两球体系统动力学的影响，推导了在均匀和径向外部场中的解析解，发现径向场中垂直于对称轴配置下两球间存在横向力分量，并分析了探测该效应所需的空间和时间灵敏度（约0.1 fm和30分钟），指出低分辨率实验也可作为强等效原理的独立检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于暗物质粒子尚未被直接探测到，修改引力理论（如MOND）作为替代解释仍具吸引力。MOND预言的外部场效应违反强等效原理，是实验检验修改引力的有力途径，但现有研究缺乏对该效应的具体动力学分析和实验可行性评估。

Method: 考虑两个球形质量，将其对称轴分别平行或正交于外部引力场排列。在强均匀外部场和径向外部场两种情况下，推导系统内部动力学的解析解。特别地，允许非相对论引力场具有非零旋度，研究径向场中垂直配置下两球间引力横向分量的存在性。同时系统评估空气阻力、物体尺寸、表面相互作用等实验效应，计算所需灵敏度。

Result: 在径向外部场中，若引力场允许非零旋度，垂直配置的两球间存在横向力分量，不与对称轴严格对齐。使用简单MOND插值函数，探测该效应需要约0.1 fm的空间灵敏度和约30分钟的时间尺度。低分辨率实验也能对修改引力理论施加约束。

Conclusion: 外部场效应可作为检验MOND等修改引力理论的实验窗口，探测该效应需极高灵敏度（亚飞米级），但通过悬浮粒子或空间实验可能实现；即使低灵敏度实验也能为强等效原理提供独立检验并约束修改引力理论。

Abstract: Despite compelling evidence, the absence of a confirmed dark matter particle has sustained interest in modified gravity as an alternative explanation for the observed phenomenology. One prominent example is Modified Newtonian Dynamics (MOND), which predicts that the internal dynamics of a system depends on the external gravitational field in which it is embedded. This so-called External Field Effect violates the strong equivalence principle (SEP) and is absent in canonical mechanics, making it a promising avenue for experimental tests of modified gravity. Motivated by this, we investigate the dynamics of two spherical masses arranged such that their symmetry axis is either parallel or orthogonal to the local gravitational field. We derive solutions describing the internal dynamics of such systems in both strong uniform and radial external fields. In particular, for a radial external field, if the non-relativistic gravitational field is free to have non-vanishing curl, we find that the mutual attraction of the masses in the perpendicular configuration is not strictly aligned with their symmetry axis. It acquires a small transverse component, even when the external gravitational field is everywhere balanced by non-gravitational forces. Using these solutions, we determine the spatial and temporal sensitivities required to distinguish the two configurations and systematically assess experimentally relevant effects, including air drag, object size, and surface interactions. As an example, detecting the prediction of the simple MOND interpolating function requires a spatial sensitivity of order 0.1 fm for sub-millimeter masses evolving over approximately 30 minutes. Such times may be achievable with levitated particles or in space-based environments. Experiments operating at lower resolutions are also interesting as independent tests of SEP and place constraints on modified-gravity theories.

</details>


### [113] [Circular orbits and particle collisions close to charged black holes surrounded by scalar clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10325)
*Maria Chivers,Betti Hartmann,Katherine Horton,Yves Brihaye*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了带标量毛的电黑洞和双黑洞时空中带电/不带电测试粒子的运动，发现标量毛的存在可产生最多四个圆形轨道（两个稳定、两个不稳定）以及静态轨道，并且粒子碰撞可产生无限质心能量，但发散时的电荷值取决于视界上的标量场值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究标量毛的存在如何改变黑洞时空中的圆形轨道和粒子碰撞特性，并与Reissner-Nordström时空进行对比。

Method: 在带标量毛的电黑洞和双黑洞时空中，分析带电/不带电测试粒子的运动方程，研究稳定与不稳定圆形轨道，以及粒子碰撞的质心能量。

Result: 标量毛存在时，最多可出现四个圆形轨道（两个稳定、两个不稳定）以及角动量L=0的静态轨道；粒子碰撞可产生无限质心能量，但发散所需电荷值受视界标量场值影响。

Conclusion: 标量毛显著改变了黑洞周围的轨道结构和碰撞特性，与RN时空存在本质差异。

Abstract: We study the motion of massive (un)charged test particles in space-times of electric and dyonic black holes which carry scalar hair. We determine the stable and unstable circular orbits and discuss the collision of massive test particles. In particular, we aim at demonstrating how the presence of the scalar hair of the black hole changes the circular orbits and particle collisions, respectively, as compared to the Reissner-Nordström (RN) space-time. We find that in the presence of scalar hair, up to four circular orbits (two unstable and two stable) as well as static orbits with $L=0$ can exist. Particle collisions can generate infinite center-of-mass energy when at least one of the particles is charged, very similar to the RN case. We find, however, that the value of the charge at which this divergence happens depends on the value of the scalar field on the horizon.

</details>


### [114] [Geodesics and shadows of the spindle-deformed Kerr black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10533)
*Hong-Da Lyu,Mingzhi Wang,Shoulong Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类新型精确Ricci平坦旋转黑洞时空中的测地线运动和黑洞阴影，该黑洞通过参数B对Kerr几何进行纺锤形变形。尽管Hamilton-Jacobi方程不可分离，但在B²阶下零测地线可分离；通过微扰方法解析确定了光子区域和黑洞阴影，并与数值射线追踪结果吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究新构造的精确Ricci平坦旋转黑洞（含纺锤形变形参数B）的测地线性质与黑洞阴影，探讨该变形对Kerr几何的偏离及其可观测效应。

Method: 采用微扰方法：在B²阶下利用零测地线的可分离性解析求解光子区域、赤道光子轨道和黑洞阴影；同时数值射线追踪验证微扰处理的有效性。

Result: 1) Hamilton-Jacobi方程对类时和零测地线均不可精确分离，但在B²阶下零测地线可分离；2) 类时测地线中，纺锤形变形移动内稳定圆轨道并可能产生外稳定圆轨道；3) 解析确定了光子区域和黑洞阴影，数值结果与微扰预测一致并量化了对Kerr的偏离。

Conclusion: 微扰方法可有效描述该黑洞时空的光学性质，零测地线在B²阶的可分离性为解析研究提供了途径；数值验证表明该处理可靠，量化了纺锤形变形对Kerr黑洞阴影的修正。

Abstract: Recently, a new exact Ricci-flat rotating black-hole solution was constructed in four-dimensional general relativity, in which an additional parameter $B$ characterizes a spindle deformation of the Kerr geometry. We study geodesic motion and black-hole shadows in this spacetime. The Hamilton-Jacobi equation is not exactly separable for either timelike or null geodesics. Remarkably, however, at the leading nontrivial order, ${\cal O}(B^2)$, null but not timelike geodesics become separable. In the timelike sector, the spindle deformation shifts the innermost stable circular orbit and can give rise to an outermost stable circular orbit. In the null sector, exploiting the perturbatively separated equations, we analytically determine the photon region, equatorial photon orbits, and black-hole shadow, and compare the resulting predictions with direct ray tracing in the exact spacetime. The numerical results validate the perturbative treatment and quantify the deviations from Kerr.

</details>


### [115] [A winding number analysis of Schwarzschild black hole stability in light of Planck-scale modified kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10600)
*Mohsen Khodadi,Nosratolla Jafari,Shahin Mamedov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文分析了普朗克尺度修正的施瓦西黑洞热力学拓扑，发现基于三次熵修正的MDR无法产生稳定的黑洞解，稳定分支因负质量/温度被排除，普朗克尺度稳定化问题仍待探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究普朗克尺度效应能否稳定黑洞，这关系到黑洞蒸发和量子引力自洽性等基本问题。

Method: 采用热力学拓扑方法，基于熵-几何对应，对施瓦西黑洞在普朗克尺度修正运动学（MDR）下进行分析，使用含ηE³/E_P主导修正的唯象MDR导出的三次熵修正，并施加物理约束条件（S'(r_h)>0, T>0）。

Result: 对于两种符号的修正参数，仅发现一个不稳定性分支（w=-1, W=-1）；第二个看似稳定的根（w=+1）因负质量/温度被排除，且位于微扰论有效范围之外。

Conclusion: 该类MDR无法产生稳定的施瓦西黑洞；不同主导阶修正的MDR可能表现不同，寻找普朗克尺度稳定化仍是一个开放问题。

Abstract: Determining whether Planck-scale effects can stabilize black holes addresses fundamental questions about black hole evaporation and quantum gravity consistency. Here, we analyze the thermodynamic topology of Schwarzschild black holes under Planck-scale modified kinematics, using a cubic entropy correction derived from a well-known phenomenological MDR with leading correction \(ηE^3/E_P\). Enforcing physical constraints (\(S'(r_h) > 0\), \(T > 0\)) via the entropy-geometry correspondence, we find a single unstable branch with \(w = -1\) and \(W = -1\) for both signs of the correction parameter. A second root suggesting stability (\(w = +1\)) is excluded due to negative mass/temperature and lies outside the perturbative regime. Thus, this class of MDRs does not yield stable Schwarzschild black holes. However, MDRs with different leading-order corrections may behave otherwise, leaving the search for Planck-scale stabilization an open endeavor.

</details>


### [116] [Einstein Frame Regularization of the JNW Spacetime: From Brans-Dicke Singularity to Ellis Wormhole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10603)
*A. Bhattacharya,R. N. Izmailov,R. Kh. Karimov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出通过复化(Wick旋转)JNW参数的方法，将全局裸奇点规则化为Ellis III类可穿越虫洞，并指出该正则化可推广到相关引力理论层级中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现对由标准爱因斯坦-最小耦合标量场(EMS)产生的JNW全局裸奇点的正则化，消除奇点问题。

Method: 对JNW参数进行复化(Wick旋转)，将原始奇异解转化为正则的二次渐近平直可穿越虫洞解。

Result: 得到的正则度规恰好是著名的Ellis III类虫洞，其源为鬼标量场(ghost EMS)。

Conclusion: JNW解属于不同引力理论的动作层级，因此其正则化版本会自动为整个引力理论层级添加对应的正则版本。

Abstract: We propose a novel way to achieve the regularization of the JNW globally naked singularity sourced by the canonical Einstein minimally coupled scalar field (EMS). The key idea is to complexify (Wick rotate) the JNW parameters, which yields a regular metric that surprisingly represents a twice asymptotically flat regular traversable wormhole, famously known as the Ellis class III wormhole sourced by the ghost EMS. We begin by arguing that the JNW solution belongs to a hierarchy of actions describing different gravity theories, which indicates that a regularized version of JNW automatically adds corresponding regular versions to the hierarchy of gravity theories.

</details>


### [117] [Scalarization of Einstein-Euler-Heisenberg black hole with multiple horizons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10614)
*Hong Guo,Yun Soo Myung*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了爱因斯坦-欧拉-海森堡黑洞在标量化理论中的多视界结构，发现磁荷在特定区间内存在四族视界和三重视界现象，并通过标量耦合构造了标量化黑洞的三条基本分支，利用质量正定性给出标量电荷上限，并通过时域稳定性分析确定了稳定存在的标量电荷窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨EEH黑洞在标量-麦克斯韦耦合下的标量化现象及其多视界结构，分析标量化黑洞的稳定性和物理可行性。

Method: 在EEH-标量理论中引入二次标量耦合到麦克斯韦项，分析磁荷EEH黑洞的视界族分类与标量化条件，通过质量正定性约束标量电荷上限，并采用时域分析方法检验稳定性。

Result: 磁荷EEH黑洞存在低、冷、负、热四族视界，三重视界出现在q∈[0.95,1.0065]窄带内。标量化黑洞存在无限分支，三条基本分支被构造。质量正定性给出低/冷视界标量电荷上限，稳定性分析表明仅在小区间出现不稳定，低/冷/热视界解分别存在中间窗口和下界稳定区域。

Conclusion: 稳定且物理可行的标量化EEH黑洞存在于标量电荷的中间窗口（低/冷视界）或下界以上区域（热视界）。

Abstract: Scalarizations of the Einstein-Euler-Heisenberg (EEH) black hole (EEHBH) with multiple horizons are investigated in the EEH-scalar theory by introducing a quadratic scalar coupling to the Maxwell term. For mass $M=1$ and Euler-Heisenberg parameter $μ=0.03$, the magnetically charged EEHBH admits four horizon families (low, cold, negative, and hot), with triple horizons appearing in the narrow band of magnetic charge $q\in[0.95,1.0065]$. The onset scalarization around the low, cold, and high horizons is then analyzed for the magnetic charge $q=0.5,\,1,\,2$, implying infinite branches of scalarized black holes for each case. We construct the three fundamental branches of scalarized black holes. From the positivity condition of their mass, we find the upper bounds on primary scalar charges $q_{s}$ for scalarized low and cold horizons. These bounds determine the allowable regions for the Hawking temperature and entropy. Furthermore, we perform a time-domain stability analysis and find that the instabilities arise only at small scalar charge regime. Therefore, stable and physically viable scalarized black holes exist in an intermediate window of the primary scalar charge for low and cold horizon solutions and a lower bound for hot horizon solution.

</details>


### [118] [Polarization-dependent observational signatures of Weyl-coupled photons around a black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10658)
*Mao-Yuan Wan,Chen Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 光子与Weyl张量的非最小耦合导致黑洞真空成为双折射介质，两种线偏振态在Schwarzschild背景上产生分裂的影子和光子环；通过波光学和几何光学方法系统计算了可观测效应，其中后向双折射信号作为最干净的诊断手段，在α=0时严格为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究光子与Weyl张量非最小耦合（一种超出广义相对论的修正引力理论）对黑洞周围光传播的影响，特别是偏振依赖的时空双折射效应及其可观测特征。

Method: 采用Riccati-Hankel匹配方案提取双偏振散射矩阵以保证相位敏感观测量的可靠性；同时使用波光学方法（灰体因子、吸收截面、微分截面及其光晕）和几何光学方法（影子、光子环、零测地线）进行统一计算，并用三个独立光线追踪器交叉验证几何光学链。

Result: 耦合参数α使两种偏振态的光子球和临界碰撞参数分裂，在α/M²=0.75时两影子边缘分离达影子半径的62%；后向双折射信号受宇称保护，在α=0时严格为零，是最干净的诊断信号。

Conclusion: 光子与Weyl张量的非最小耦合产生的偏振依赖双折射效应可通过黑洞阴影分裂、光子环以及后向散射信号等可观测量进行检验，为探测修正引力理论提供了新的观测途径。

Abstract: A photon coupled non-minimally to the Weyl tensor propagates differently in its two linear polarization states: the vacuum around a black hole becomes a birefringent medium. On a Schwarzschild background the coupling $α$ splits the photon sphere and the critical impact parameter of the two polarizations, producing a double shadow and a polarization-dependent photon ring; the two shadow edges separate by $62\%$ of the shadow radius at $α/M^2=0.75$. We compute a unified set of wave-optical and geometric-optical observables for both polarizations across a range of $α$: greybody factors, absorption cross sections, differential cross sections and their glories, a backward birefringence signal, shadows, photon rings, and null trajectories. The two-polarization scattering matrices are extracted with a Riccati--Hankel matching scheme that keeps the phase-sensitive observables reliable, and the geometric-optics chain is cross-validated with three independent ray tracers. The cleanest diagnostic is the backward signal: it is protected by parity and vanishes identically at $α=0$.

</details>


### [119] [Corrections to inflationary models induced by non-minimal coupling between scalar field and curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10679)
*I. V. Fomin,S. V. Chervon,E. S. Dentsel,B. Mishra*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种标量场与曲率非最小耦合的参数化方案，研究其对暴胀模型特征的修正。这些修正导致暴胀阶段标量场势能变形及宇宙学扰动参数偏差，同时该参数化下再加热阶段动力学与爱因斯坦引力中最小耦合情况完全一致。通过张标比对标量谱指数的级数展开，发展了一种模型无关的暴胀修正分析方法，并将已知暴胀模型作为特例纳入其中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非最小耦合对标量场-曲率相互作用的参数化描述，以更统一地理解其对暴胀模型特征（势能形状、扰动参数、再加热动力学）的影响，并建立一种不依赖具体模型的系统分析框架。

Method: 采用特定参数化方案描述非最小耦合，分析其对暴胀势能的形变效应；利用张标比r与标量谱指数n_s之间的级数展开关系，对暴胀修正进行模型无关的分类研究。

Result: 非最小耦合参数化导致暴胀势能变形，使宇宙学扰动参数（如张标比、谱指数）偏离最小耦合情形；再加热阶段动力学完全等价于爱因斯坦引力最小耦合情况；所提出的级数展开方法能将多种已知暴胀模型作为特例包含。

Conclusion: 该参数化方案为研究非最小耦合暴胀修正提供了统一框架，既能描述暴胀与再加热阶段的物理，又通过模型无关的分类方法兼容了现有暴胀模型。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider possible corrections to the characteristics of inflationary models based on a specific parametrization of the non-minimal coupling between the scalar field and curvature. At the inflationary stage, these corrections lead to a deformation of the scalar field potential and a corresponding deviation in the determination of the cosmological perturbation parameters. At the same time, it is shown that the proposed parametrization yields a description of the reheating stage dynamics completely analogous to the case of Einstein gravity with minimal coupling between the scalar field and curvature. For a model-independent analysis of inflationary corrections induced by a non-minimal coupling, a classification of inflationary scenarios based on the expansion in series of the dependence of the tensor-to-scalar ratio on the spectral index of scalar perturbations is considered. It is also shown that this approach allows for the inclusion of well-known inflationary models as special cases.

</details>


### [120] [Regular black holes with Minkowskian cores: causal structure and observational degeneracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10713)
*Francisco Tello-Ortiz,Y. Gómez-Leyton,Alejandro Rueda,Kazuharu Bamba,Manuel González-Espinoza*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一类具有闵可夫斯基核心和双视界结构的渐进平坦静态与旋转规则黑洞，发现尽管其内部几何与奇点黑洞存在显著差异，但光学特征（阴影和吸积盘成像）与相同渐近参数的Schwarzschild/Kerr黑洞几乎不可区分，表明当前阴影观测难以探测黑洞内部结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造具有规则内部（无奇点）且满足渐近平坦性的解析黑洞解，并检验其是否可通过光学观测与经典奇点黑洞区分。

Method: 基于引力退耦框架构造静态和旋转规则黑洞解；通过阴影可观测量和几何薄、光学薄吸积盘的射线追踪成像分析其光学表现。

Result: 尽管内部几何差异巨大，这些规则黑洞的阴影和吸积盘图像与Schwarzschild/Kerr黑洞几乎一致。

Conclusion: 当前基于阴影和成像的观测难以探测黑洞的内部结构，即便内部几何截然不同的黑洞其光学外观也可能几乎一致。

Abstract: We construct a new family of asymptotically flat static and rotating regular black holes characterized by a Minkowskian core and a finite two-horizon structure. The solutions are obtained within the framework of gravitational decoupling and provide an analytically tractable realization of non-singular black hole geometries with a regular interior and well-defined asymptotic properties. We investigate the optical appearance of the rotating spacetime through shadow observables and ray-traced images of geometrically thin, optically thin accretion disks. Despite the substantial differences between the interior geometry of these solutions and that of singular black holes, their optical signatures are found to remain remarkably close to those of Schwarzschild and Kerr spacetimes with the same asymptotic parameters. Our results show that markedly different black hole interiors may lead to nearly indistinguishable optical appearances, highlighting the challenges of probing the internal structure of compact objects using current shadow and imaging observations.

</details>


### [121] [Nonlinear electrodynamics and stability of spherically symmetric space-times in scalar-tensor gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10718)
*Kirill A. Bronnikov,Rustam Ibadov,Feruza Y. Shaymanova,Najibullokhon M. Shukurullokhon*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Bergmann-Wagoner-Nordtvedt类标量-张量引力理论中非线性电动力学（NED）源下的静态球对称解的线性扰动，推导了有效势的普适表达式，并证明在麦克斯韦弱场极限下，零电荷极限的有效势不包含NED痕迹；同时，对于等电量磁荷（F=0）情形，STT-NED解的稳定性结果与STT-Maxwell解一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展标量-张量引力理论中非线性电动力学源解的稳定性分析，填补此前仅限于麦克斯韦线性电动力学情形的理论空白，并考察标量-电磁任意相互作用形式下的扰动行为。

Method: 采用线性径向（单极）扰动方法，针对具有标量-电磁相互作用L(ψ, F)的广义STT-NED系统，推导有效势V_eff的通用表达式；通过分析零电荷极限和等电荷极限，对比STT-Maxwell情形的稳定性结论。

Result: (1) 得到了任意标量-电磁相互作用下径向扰动的有效势普适公式；(2) 当NED具有正确的麦克斯韦弱场极限时，零电荷极限下的有效势完全不含NED贡献，扰动动力学退化为真空STT情形；(3) 对于等电量磁荷（F=0）的STT-NED解，其稳定性结果与STT-Maxwell解完全相同。

Conclusion: STT-NED系统的径向扰动稳定性分析可归约为已知的STT-Maxwell情形，只要满足弱场极限条件或等电荷条件，且有效势的通用表达式为后续研究提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We study linear perturbations of static, spherically symmetric solutions of scalar-tensor theories (STT) of gravity from the Bergmann-Wagoner-Nordtvedt class, sourced by nonlinear electrodynamics (NED). We obtain a general expression for the effective potential $V_{\rm eff}$ governing the perturbation dynamics for theories with arbitrary scalar-electromagnetic interaction of the form $L(ψ, F)$, where $ψ$ is a scalar field and $F = F_{μν} F^{μν}$ the electromagnetic invariant. This consideration includes, in particular, arbitrary scalar self-interaction potentials and scalar fields that can be phantom in some regions of space-time (the so-called trapped ghosts). Only radial (monopole) perturbations are considered here as the most likely ones to cause an instability. It is shown, in particular, that if NED has a correct Maxwell weak field limit, the zero charge limit of $V_{\rm eff}$ does not contain any trace of NED, and the perturbation dynamics is the same as for vacuum STT solutions. The previously obtained stability results for STT-Maxwell solutions are shown to be extended without change to STT-NED solutions with equal electric and magnetic charges, implying $F =0$.

</details>


### [122] [Scalar-induced gravitational waves from a box-shaped curvature power spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10730)
*Zu-Cheng Chen,Lang Liu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文计算了由平坦原初曲率功率谱在辐射主导时期产生的二阶随机引力波背景，将功率谱建模为有限波段上的对数箱型分布，解析推导了窄箱和宽箱两种极限下的引力波谱，并给出了统一的解析公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了研究介于单色谱和标度不变平台之间的中间情况，作者构造了对数箱型原初功率谱，其宽度参数Δ可插值两种极限，从而系统研究原初谱形状对二阶引力波谱的影响。

Method: 在辐射主导时期，将动量卷积积分简化为动量三角形与箱型区域重叠的紧凑积分，分别对窄箱（小Δ）和宽箱（大Δ）做解析处理，并构造了统一的闭合解析公式。

Result: 窄箱情形下，谱主项为狄拉克δ谱乘以几何重叠因子Φ_box(κ,Δ)，红外斜率从k³ln²k变为k²ln²k；宽箱情形下，谱分为下边缘（k³ln²k上升）、标度不变平台和上边缘（四次方截止），并给出了统一的乘积型闭合公式。

Conclusion: 对数箱模型可解析连接单色谱和标度不变谱两种极限，所得到的统一公式可直接用于参数扫描和引力波背景的观测预测。

Abstract: We compute the stochastic background of gravitational waves (GWs) produced at second order in cosmological perturbation theory by a primordial curvature power spectrum that is flat in $\ln k$ over a finite band $[k_-,k_+]=[k_*e^{-Δ},k_*e^{+Δ}]$ and vanishes elsewhere. This logarithmic box interpolates between a monochromatic spectrum as $Δ\to0$ and a locally scale-invariant plateau as $Δ\to\infty$, and its sharp boundaries make the geometry of the source convolution unusually transparent. Working in the radiation era, we reduce the convolution to a compact integral over the overlap between the momentum triangle and the box, and evaluate it analytically in two regimes. For a narrow box we show that, to leading order in the width, the spectrum equals the Dirac-spectrum result multiplied by a purely geometric overlap factor $Φ_{\rm box}(κ,Δ)$; this factor turns the infrared slope from $k^{3}\ln^{2}k$ into $k^{2}\ln^{2}k$ at a break $k_b=2k_*\sinhΔ$. For a broad box we separate the lower edge, the scale-invariant interior, and the upper edge, derive the leading behaviour in each (an infrared $k^{3}\ln^{2}k$ rise, a scale-invariant plateau, and a quartic cut-off at the hard endpoint $k=2k_+$), and combine them into a single uniform formula cast as a product of two universal, $Δ$-independent edge functions. We also provide an integral-free closed-form surrogate for these edge functions for use in parameter scans.

</details>


### [123] [On correlated noise in the LIGO-Virgo network: a test of the stochastic gravitational-wave background hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10816)
*S. C. Ulhoa,B. C. C. Carneiro,F. L. Carneiro,L. V. A. Cunha*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了引力波验证中的探测器间互相关准则，将Creswell等人的分析扩展到LIGO-Virgo O2和O3运行期间的9个候选事件。对每个事件的三个探测器对（H1×L1、H1×V1、L1×V1）进行分析，在27次测量中，26次的事件峰值与噪声统计上不可区分，唯一例外是GW190412的H1×L1对。结论是互相关统计量不能独立可靠地区分事件与噪声，而持续的相关结构可能源于随机引力波背景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新检验互相关准则作为引力波验证标准的可靠性，探究LIGO-Virgo数据中持续存在的相关结构的物理起源。

Method: 将Creswell等人的分析方法扩展到O2和O3的9个候选事件，对每个事件分析三个探测器对的归一化互相关函数C(τ)，并与N=200个周围离源窗口的经验系综进行比较，使用仅带通滤波的处理流程。

Result: 在27次测量中，26次的事件峰值与噪声系综统计上不可区分，仅GW190412的H1×L1对表现出异常。

Conclusion: 互相关统计量不能作为独立可靠的事件-噪声区分标准；LIGO-Virgo探测器对间持续存在的相关结构暗示应变数据中可能包含连续的物理相关成分，随机引力波背景是一个合理的候选解释。

Abstract: We revisit the inter-detector cross-correlation criterion used in gravitational-wave validation by extending the analysis of Creswell et al.~\cite{Creswell2017} to nine LIGO--Virgo candidate events from the O2 and O3 observing runs. For each event, we analyze all three detector pairs (H1$\times$L1, H1$\times$V1, L1$\times$V1) and compare the normalized cross-correlation function $C(τ)$ in the event window with an empirical ensemble of $N=200$ surrounding off-source windows, using a band-pass-only pipeline. In 26 of 27 pair-measurements, the event peak is statistically indistinguishable from the corresponding noise ensemble, including all pairs involving the independent Virgo detector. The only exception is GW190412 in the H1$\times$L1 pair. We conclude that the cross-correlation statistic does not provide a robust standalone separation between event and noise. We interpret the persistence of comparable correlation structure in the LIGO--Virgo pairs as evidence that the strain data may contain a continuous correlated physical component, for which a stochastic gravitational-wave background is a plausible candidate.

</details>


### [124] [Comparing twist-free axisymmetric gravitational waves near the black hole threshold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10843)
*Ananya Adhikari,Tomáš Ledvinka,Daniela Cors,Thomas W. Baumgarte,Bernd Brügmann,David Hilditch*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过三个独立数值代码（bamps、prague和sphGR）对轴对称真空引力塌缩中的黑洞形成阈值进行了详细比较研究，重点分析了近阈值塌缩中曲率标量的准普适强场特征，并首次在bamps代码中演化时间非对称波初始数据，三者结果高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴对称真空引力中黑洞形成的阈值比预期的更为复杂，需要多个独立代码和方法之间的交叉验证以达成共识。

Method: 采用三个独立数值代码（bamps、prague和sphGR）进行对比，构建参考坐标系以比较对称轴上的几何特征，首次在bamps代码中演化时间非对称波初始数据，并分析表观视界及强场时空分类的困难。

Result: 三个代码的结果高度一致；观测到曲率标量中出现准普适的强场特征；首次成功在bamps代码中演化时间非对称波初始数据。

Conclusion: 三个独立代码在近阈值引力塌缩模拟中达成良好共识，为理解轴对称真空引力中黑洞形成的复杂性提供了可靠基础。

Abstract: The threshold of black hole formation in axisymmetric vacuum gravity is proving to be more complicated than had been anticipated but, following recent advances, a consensus between independent codes and methods is emerging. Building on earlier work we provide further details of a comparison between three independent numerical codes (the bamps, prague, and sphGR codes), paying special attention to the relative strengths and weaknesses of each and examining various features of near-threshold collapse of vacuum gravitational waves for the first time. In particular, we observe quasi-universal strong-field features appearing in curvature scalars. Focusing on geometric features on the symmetry axis, we construct reference coordinates to aid the comparison of strong-field data. We evolve, for the first time, time-asymmetric wave initial data within the bamps code. To the extent possible with current methods we compare apparent horizons and attempt to determine what causes difficulties in the classification of these strong-field and highly dynamical spacetimes. In all cases the results from the three codes agree very well.

</details>


### [125] [Post-Newtonian N-Body Dynamics in Extended Theories of Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10889)
*Antonio Tedesco*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文推导了标量-张量-四阶引力（STFOG）中相对论性N体系统的1PN拉格朗日量和运动方程，包含非交换谱几何（NCSG）作为特例。在Φ∼Ψ（γ∼1）条件下，求解了线性化四阶场方程，构建了基于点粒子作用的变分拉格朗日量。动力学由三个Yukawa函数（ζ、𝒲、Ξ）控制，分别对应标量、引力磁和三体扇区，依赖于有效质量(m_R,m_Y,m_φ)。证明了非线性度规分量⁽⁴⁾g₀₀在1PN阶不起作用，给出了闭式轨道运动，并在广义相对论极限下恢复了Einstein-Infeld-Hoffmann方程。该形式为太阳系、双脉冲星（如PSR J0737-3039）、银河系中心恒星轨道和三体系统的相对论天体力学提供了统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立STFOG（含NCSG）中完整的1PN N体动力学理论，为检验引力理论的太阳系、脉冲星和银河系中心实验提供理论基础。

Method: 在标准后牛顿规范下求解线性化四阶场方程，通过点粒子作用量直接构建变分拉格朗日量，引入三个Yukawa函数参数化标量、引力磁和三体修正。

Result: 获得了由三个Yukawa函数控制的闭式1PN轨道运动方程；证明⁽⁴⁾g₀₀在1PN阶无贡献；在GR极限下恢复EIH方程。

Conclusion: 该形式为STFOG/NCSG中的相对论天体力学提供了统一框架，可应用于太阳系、双脉冲星、银河系恒星轨道和三体系统的高精度引力检验。

Abstract: We derive the complete first post-Newtonian (1PN) Lagrangian and corresponding equations of motion for the relativistic $N$-body system in Scalar-Tensor-Fourth-Order Gravity (STFOG), including the Non-Commutative Spectral Geometry (NCSG) sector as a special case. In the regime $Φ\sim Ψ$ ($γ\sim 1$), the linearized fourth-order field equations are solved in the Standard Post-Newtonian gauge, and the variational Lagrangian is built directly from the point-particle action. The resulting dynamics is governed by three Yukawa functions $ζ$, $\mathcal{W}$ and $Ξ$, which encode the scalar, gravitomagnetic and three-body sectors and depend on the effective masses $(m_R,m_Y,m_φ)$ of the additional propagating modes. In this context, we show that the nonlinear metric component ${}^{(4)}\!g_{00}$ plays no role at 1PN level. The 1PN orbital motion of the above extended theories is thus obtained in closed form, and the Einstein--Infeld--Hoffmann equations are recovered in the corresponding general-relativistic limit. The formalism provides a common framework for the relativistic celestial mechanics of the Solar System, binary pulsars such as PSR J0737-3039, Galactic-center stellar orbits and triple systems.

</details>


### [126] [How a minimal length scale modifies thermodynamics of RN AdS Black Holes?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10964)
*Suman Kumar Panja,Yu Shi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在κ-形变时空背景下，最小长度尺度对Reissner-Nordström反德西特（RN AdS）黑洞热力学的修正，包括霍金温度、熵、焓、热稳定性、P-V临界性以及焦耳-汤姆孙膨胀，发现非对易参数系统性地变形了相共存区域和反转曲线，但整体仍保持与范德瓦尔斯流体的热力学类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子引力在黑洞热力学中的可观测信号，通过在κ-形变时空中引入最小长度尺度，研究其对RN AdS黑洞热力学性质的修正效应。

Method: 通过相空间对易关系构造修正度规，推导变形后的霍金温度、熵和焓；利用热容分析热稳定性；通过P-V临界性研究相变行为；分析焦耳-汤姆孙膨胀过程。

Result: 黑洞经历与范德瓦尔斯系统类似的小-大相变，但临界比因非对易性略有降低；非对易参数在温度-压力平面中扩大了冷却区域；共存区域和反转曲线被系统性变形。

Conclusion: 最小长度尺度系统性地修正了RN AdS黑洞的热力学性质，尽管与范德瓦尔斯流体的热力学类比仍然成立，但这些变形为量子引力提供了潜在的可观测信号。

Abstract: We investigate the thermodynamic modifications of the Reissner-Nordstroem anti-de Sitter (RN AdS) black hole induced by a minimal length scale,which naturally emerges in $κ$-deformed space-time. By constructing the modified metric via phase-space commutation relations,we derive the deformed Hawking temperature, entropy, and enthalpy. We analyze the thermal stability through the heat capacity and study the P-V criticality, revealing that the black hole undergoes a small-to-large phase transition analogous to the Van der Waals system, albeit with a critical ratio slightly lowered by non-commutativity. Furthermore, we examine the Joule-Thomson expansion and find that the non-commutative (NC) parameter expands the cooling region in the temperature-pressure plane. Our results demonstrate that while the overall thermodynamic analogy with the Van der Waals fluid persists, the minimal length scale systematically deform the coexistence region and inversion curves, offering potential observational signatures for quantum gravity.

</details>


### [127] [A Space Mission to Earth's Nearest Black Hole: Reality or Science Fiction?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10982)
*Cosimo Bambi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了向最近黑洞发射星际小型航天器的前景，尽管高度推测且充满技术挑战，但并非完全不可能。所需技术可能在20-30年内实现，任务周期约80-100年，但科学回报将极为深远且难以通过其他方式获得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞是宇宙中最强引力场的来源，为检验爱因斯坦广义相对论的强场极限提供了无与伦比的机会，现有观测手段难以直接探测黑洞近邻区域。

Method: 提出向最近黑洞发射小型星际航天器的概念性任务方案，评估其技术可行性与时间框架。

Result: 所需技术可能在20-30年内成熟；任务周期预计80-100年。

Conclusion: 尽管任务高度推测且技术挑战巨大，但科学回报极为深远，且可能无法通过其他手段实现。

Abstract: Black holes are the sources of the strongest gravitational fields in the present-day Universe, offering unparalleled opportunities to test Einstein's theory of General Relativity in the strong-field regime. In this talk, I will examine the prospect of sending small spacecraft on an interstellar mission to the nearest black hole. While highly speculative and fraught with technical challenges, such an endeavor is not entirely beyond the realm of possibility. Although the necessary technology does not yet exist, it may become available within the next 20 to 30 years. The mission itself could span 80 to 100 years, yet the scientific return would be profound and potentially unattainable through other means.

</details>


### [128] [Spinoptics in the presence of axion-like particles in curved spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11134)
*Tomoki Takeuchi,Tsutomu Kobayashi*

TL;DR: 本文研究高频电磁波与轴子标量场在弯曲时空中的耦合，通过有效作用量方法推导了超越标准几何光学近似的轴子-麦克斯韦理论的自旋光学方程，分析了光子在轴子场和时空曲率作用下轨道的螺旋性相关修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究轴子场与电磁波耦合在弯曲时空中的高阶光学效应，超越标准几何光学极限，揭示光子自旋与轴子场及时空曲率的相互作用对传播轨迹的影响。

Method: 采用有效作用量方法，推导适用于任意弯曲时空和任意轴子分布的轴子-麦克斯韦理论自旋光学方程。

Result: 获得了考虑螺旋性依赖修正的光子轨迹方程，并通过代表性示例展示了光路在轴子场存在下偏离零测地线的自旋光学效应。

Conclusion: 轴子场与电磁波耦合会引入螺旋性依赖的光子轨迹修正，使光线偏离标准零测地线路径。

Abstract: We study the propagation of high-frequency electromagnetic waves coupled to an axionic scalar field in curved spacetime. By applying the effective action approach, we go beyond the standard geometric optics limit and derive spinoptics equations for the axion--Maxwell theory that are valid for arbitrary curved spacetimes and arbitrary axion profiles. These equations allow us to analyze helicity-dependent corrections to photon trajectories arising from the photon's interactions with both the axion field and spacetime curvature. We present representative examples to demonstrate how light trajectories deviate from null geodesics due to spinoptics effects in the presence of an axion field.

</details>


### [129] [Formation of Trapped Surfaces from Spacelike Initial Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11236)
*Xuantao Chen,Sergiu Klainerman*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用自由数据形式体系，构造了短脉冲型柯西数据，并通过前向积分将其扩展为渐近平坦的柯西数据集，显著推广了Li-Yu的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 利用经典文献CK25和CK26中发展的自由数据形式体系，基于CK26中的局部存在性结果构建类空短脉冲数据，并通过CK26中的前向积分构造方法实现数据扩展。

Result: 成功构造了短脉冲型柯西数据，并证明该数据可以扩展为一组渐近平坦的柯西数据集。

Conclusion: 该工作显著推广了Li-Yu的先前结果。

Abstract: We make use of the free data formalism developed in \cite{CK25,CK26} to provide a direct construction of short-pulse type Cauchy data. The construction of the spacelike short-pulse follows from the local existence result established in \cite{CK26}. The forward integration construction in \cite{CK26} allows us to show that such data can be extended to a set of asymptotically flat Cauchy data. This greatly extends the result of Li--Yu \cite{LiYu}.

</details>


### [130] [Turbulence, Norm Inflation, and the Rendall Instability for the Einstein-Euler System with Positive Cosmological Constant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11375)
*Elliot Marshall*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文首次在三维空间中数值研究了具有正宇宙常数和线性状态方程 p=Kρ (K∈(1/3,1)) 的爱因斯坦-欧拉方程的扰动。研究发现Rendall不稳定性会导致流体能量密度出现前向湍流级联和H^s范数膨胀，这是已知的第一个具有正宇宙常数的场方程解的小数据湍流实例，并可能驱动晚期宇宙的结构形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对Rendall不稳定性的研究仅限于一维空间，本文首次在一维以上空间（三维）中研究该不稳定性的发生机制，并探索其是否会导致湍流现象以及宇宙结构形成。

Method: 采用数值方法研究具有正宇宙常数和线性状态方程 p=Kρ (K∈(1/3,1)) 的爱因斯坦-欧拉方程的时空均匀且正交解的微扰，特别关注U(1)对称扰动下的能量密度演化。

Result: 发现Rendall不稳定性导致前向湍流级联和H^s范数膨胀；这是具有正宇宙常数的场方程解中首次观察到小数据湍流；该不稳定性可驱动晚期宇宙结构形成。

Conclusion: Rendall不稳定性不仅在三维空间中存在，而且能引发湍流现象和能量密度范数膨胀，对理解晚期宇宙结构形成具有重要意义。

Abstract: We numerically study perturbations of spatially homogeneous and orthogonal solutions to the Einstein-Euler equations with positive cosmological constant and linear equation of state $p=Kρ$ for $K \in (\frac{1}{3},1)$. Recent numerical and analytic work has demonstrated that the fluid density gradient in these perturbed spacetimes can form steep gradients and blow up as future timelike infinity is approached, a phenomenon now known as the `Rendall instability'. In this article, we study the onset of this instability outside of one spatial dimension for the first time. Our results provide compelling evidence that the Rendall instability causes a forward turbulent cascade and $H^{s}$ norm inflation in the energy density of the fluid from arbitrarily small $U(1)$-symmetric perturbations. This is, to the best of our knowledge, the first example of small data turbulence for solutions of the field equations with a positive cosmological constant. Moreover, we find that this instability can drive structure formation in the late universe.

</details>


### [131] [Sonic black holes with thick horizons in BECs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11396)
*Daniel Peñalver,Marco De Vito,Roberto Balbinot,Alessandro Fabbri*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究具有延伸声速区域的声学黑洞模型（厚视界黑洞），分析了粒子产生数和密度-密度关联函数，后者是实验识别声学黑洞中霍金辐射的关键工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有厚视界（延伸声速区域）的声学黑洞模型，以更贴近实际实验条件，并通过密度-密度关联函数为实验探测霍金辐射提供理论依据。

Method: 采用一维阶梯均匀流动的玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体模型，考虑存在延伸声速区域（声速等于流速的区域）的情况，计算粒子产生数和密度-密度关联函数。

Result: 得到了厚视界声学黑洞模型中粒子产生数的理论结果，并推导了密度-密度关联函数的特征模式，这些模式可作为实验识别霍金辐射的信号。

Conclusion: 厚视界声学黑洞模型在实验上更易实现，其密度-密度关联函数可作为有效工具探测类霍金辐射，为声学黑洞实验提供了可检验的理论预测。

Abstract: We consider simple one dimensional models of acoustic black holes formed by Bose-Einstein condensates where the flow is stepwise homogeneous. We shall concentrate on the case in which an extended sonic region is present, i.e. a region where the flow velocity equals exactly the speed of sound. These models can be thought of as acoustic black holes with a thick horizon. Particular attention will be devoted to the number of created particles and to the density-density correlation functions, these latter being at present time the experimental tool to identify Hawking like radiation in acoustic black holes.

</details>


### [132] [Dynamics and observational signatures of warm Dirac-Born-Infeld inflation with nonminimal derivative coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11519)
*Run-Qing Zhao,Xiao-Min Zhang,Peng-Cheng Chu,Yun-Cai Feng*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有非最小导数耦合（NMDC）的暖DBI暴胀模型，发现NMDC引起的引力摩擦与热耗散共同作用可有效调节标量谱指数、显著扩展可行参数空间，并将张标比压制在10⁻⁸~10⁻⁵范围，同时缓解η问题并避免超普朗克场位移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非正则DBI动力学、NMDC引力摩擦与热耗散三者结合对暴胀模型的影响，检验该模型在观测数据下的可行性，并解决标准暴胀中的η问题和超普朗克场位移问题。

Method: 建立含NMDC的暖DBI暴胀背景演化方程及慢滚稳定性条件，推导标量谱指数n_s和张标比R的解析结果，并利用Planck 2018数据对n=2和n=4幂律势的参数空间进行约束。

Result: NMDC引力摩擦与热耗散相互作用有效调节n_s并显著扩展可行参数空间；N=50的预测落入95%置信区间，N=60进入68%置信区间并接近观测中心值；张标比被显著压制在10⁻⁸~10⁻⁵范围；联合阻尼机制放松了η慢滚条件并限制暴胀子场位移。

Conclusion: 暖DBI暴胀与NMDC结合提供了一个理论自洽且观测可行的暴胀模型，展示了热耗散与增强引力摩擦在修正引力背景下的互补效应。

Abstract: This paper investigates a warm Dirac-Born-Infeld (DBI) inflationary model with nonminimal derivative coupling (NMDC) to gravity, where the inflaton kinetic term interacts with the Einstein tensor, thereby improving the effective gravitational friction. This model seamlessly integrates the noncanonical DBI kinetic structure, the NMDC-induced gravitational friction, and thermal dissipation. We formulate the background evolution equations along with the corresponding slow-roll stability conditions, leading to analytic results for the scalar spectral index $n_s$ and the tensor-to-scalar ratio $R$. By applying these results to power-law potentials with $n=2$ and $n=4$, the model parameter space is constrained using the \emph{Planck} 2018 data. The findings indicate that the interaction between NMDC-induced gravitational friction and thermal dissipation effectively modulates $n_s$ and significantly expands the viable parameter space. In the $(n_s,R)$ plane, the predictions for $N=50$ fall within the 95\% confidence-level region, while those for $N=60$ extend into the 68\% confidence-level region and approach the observationally preferred central values. For the representative parameter choices examined, the tensor-to-scalar ratio is notably suppressed, generally within the range $10^{-8}\lesssim R\lesssim10^{-5}$. Moreover, the combined damping mechanism relaxes the slow-roll condition related to $η$ and limits the inflaton field excursion, thus addressing the $η$ problem without incurring super-Planckian field variations. These results indicate that warm DBI inflation with NMDC offers a theoretically coherent and observationally viable inflationary model, showcasing the complementary effects of thermal dissipation and enhanced gravitational friction in the context of modified gravity.

</details>


### [133] [Black-hole evaporation from the semiclassical Einstein equations in 3+1 dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11737)
*Adrián del Río,F. Javier Marañón-González*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过重新表述Hadamard重整化方案，首次在3+1维中获得了蒸发Schwarzschild黑洞的半经典爱因斯坦方程的闭式解析解，揭示了类时视界结构，为研究黑洞蒸发因果结构和信息丢失问题提供了新的解析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四维半经典引力中缺乏描述黑洞蒸发的解析解，此前仅二维有效模型具备解析可控性，亟需在真实四维时空建立半经典反作用的解析框架。

Method: 重新表述Hadamard重整化方案，避免标准模和构造，直接得到3+1维弯曲时空中标量量子场的显式应力-能量张量；构造Unruh态，再现未来零无穷远处的霍金辐射通量和视界处负能内流。

Result: 获得了四维半经典引力中首个描述黑洞蒸发的解析准稳态解；所得几何呈现类时表观视界。

Conclusion: 该工作将此前仅二维有效的解析控制扩展到四维，为研究黑洞蒸发的因果结构和信息丢失问题开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We solve the semiclassical Einstein equations for an evaporating Schwarzschild black hole in closed form, obtaining its backreacted geometry in 3+1 dimensions. This is achieved by a reformulation of the Hadamard renormalization prescription, which admits explicit stress-energy tensors for scalar quantum fields in 3+1-dimensional curved spacetimes without the standard mode-sum construction. The Unruh-like states we construct reproduce the Hawking flux at future null infinity and the associated ingoing negative-energy flux at the horizon, yielding the first analytical quasi-stationary solution describing black-hole evaporation in four-dimensional semiclassical gravity. The resulting geometry exhibits a timelike apparent horizon, providing a new analytical setting to study the causal structure of black-hole evaporation and its connection with the information-loss issue. Our results open a new route to semiclassical backreaction in four dimensions, extending the level of analytical control previously available only in two-dimensional effective models.

</details>


### [134] [A way to constrain a graviton mass from astronomical observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11764)
*Alexander F. Zakharov,Predrag Jovanovic,Dusko Borka,Vesna Borka Jovanovic*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文综述了有质量引力理论中引力子质量的各种天文观测限制方法，包括LIGO引力波信号分析、银河系中心恒星轨道观测（GRAVITY和Keck组数据），以及其他天文学途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有质量引力理论作为广义相对论的替代理论近年来受到广泛关注，但其早期版本存在缺陷。获取引力子质量的天文观测限制对检验这些理论至关重要。

Method: 作者综合分析了多种天文观测途径对引力子质量的限制：(1) LIGO对双黑洞合并引力波信号波形的分析；(2) GRAVITY和Keck观测组对银河系中心亮星轨道的追踪数据；(3) 简要讨论其他天文学方法。

Result: 通过引力波信号分析和恒星轨道观测获得了引力子质量的限制值，多种天文观测方法均能对引力子质量给出有意义的约束。

Conclusion: 多种互补的天文观测手段可以为有质量引力理论中的引力子质量提供有效的观测限制，未来更多观测数据有望进一步收紧这些限制。

Abstract: Along with a whole range of alternative theories of gravity, variants of the massive theory of gravity, i.e. the theory of gravity, in which the graviton has mass, have been actively discussed in recent years. Theorists have proposed versions of massive gravity theories that address the shortcomings of early versions of such theories. Astrophysicists and experimental physicists have been discussing limitations on the graviton mass from various astronomical observations. In particular, in the first LIGO paper, where the discovery of gravitational waves from binary black holes was reported, a limit on the mass of the graviton was obtained from the analysis of the profile of the gravitational wave signal. In the paper graviton mass constraints were obtained by analyzing the trajectory of a bright star in the vicinity of the center of our Galaxy, using observations from the GRAVITY and Keck groups. Briefly other astronomical ways to limit a graviton mass were discussed.

</details>


### [135] [Is there ghost and tachyon free bounce in UV complete gravity theory?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11765)
*Hao Hu,Alexey S. Koshelev,Abhishek Naskar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究解析无限导数引力理论中的反弹宇宙解，发现一般条件下无法构造无鬼影或快子不稳定性的反弹解，除非引入负宇宙常数。已知反弹解中高阶导数形式因子的解析性与解参数结合会导致鬼影辐射。标量模式对CMB观测无影响，张量模式谱被计算并讨论了相关影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在解析无限导数引力框架中是否可能实现没有鬼影或快子不稳定性问题的反弹宇宙解，旨在解决宇宙学奇点问题。

Method: 在(1+3)形式下对闵可夫斯基和德西特时空中的自由度进行详细分析，并解析已知反弹解中的高阶导数形式因子的解析性与解参数关系。

Result: 一般基础上无法构造无不稳定性的反弹解（除非有负宇宙常数）；已知反弹解中存在鬼影辐射；标量模式不影响CMB观测；张量模式谱被计算。

Conclusion: 在解析无限导数引力框架中，无奇点的反弹宇宙解通常伴随不稳定性问题，标量模式对CMB无影响，张量模式则需进一步考虑其观测含义。

Abstract: Analytic infinite derivative gravity theories provide a renormalizable and ghost-free description of gravity around covariantly constant backgrounds. These theories can have non-singular bouncing Universe solutions. In this paper we aim to address a question whether it is possible to realize a bouncing solution without the presence of a ghost or a tachyon instability in this framework. We perform a detailed analysis of degrees of freedom in $(1+3)$ formalism around Minkowski and de Sitter space-times. As a result it becomes clear that on a very general basis one cannot construct an instability free bounce without a negative cosmological constant. An analysis of known bouncing solutions in this model shows that an analyticity of higher derivative form factors in combination with solutions parameters result in the presence of a ghost radiation. Being motivated by the idea of resolving the cosmological singularity problem we proceed by analyzing scalar and tensor modes anyway. Scalar modes appear to not influence Cosmic Microwave Background observations at all, while tensor modes spectrum is computed and the corresponding implications are discussed.

</details>


### [136] [Universal Bounds on Black Hole Observables Imposed by Energy Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11840)
*Rahul Kumar Walia,Boris Georgiev,Chi-kwan Chan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文建立了经典能量条件与可观测黑洞性质（阴影大小、Lyapunov指数/时间、光子环时间延迟）之间的普适联系，为爱因斯坦引力下静态球对称黑洞提供了不依赖于具体解或物质模型的解析边界，可用于现有及未来地平线尺度观测检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在将经典能量条件（通常作为理论假设）转化为可直接观测检验的黑洞强场引力探针，弥补现有黑洞阴影观测缺少与物质模型无关的普适理论边界的不足。

Method: 在光子球面处局域施加经典能量条件（弱、强、主导能量条件），利用广义相对论下静态球对称黑洞度规的几何与物质场关系，推导出阴影半径、Lyapunov指数、Lyapunov时间及光子环时间延迟的解析边界，并以度规函数或应力-能动张量局部分量两种等价形式表达。

Result: 得到了与具体黑洞解或物质模型无关的通用边界；这些边界可直接应用于当前（如EHT）及未来（如ngEHT）的地平线尺度观测；任何观测违反这些边界将意味着经典能量条件被破坏或广义相对论在强场区域失效。

Conclusion: 经典能量条件不仅是理论假设，更可作为强场引力的观测探针；该工作为检验黑洞候选者是否与爱因斯坦引力及合理物质场兼容提供了系统框架。

Abstract: We establish a universal connection between the classical energy conditions and directly observable black hole properties. By imposing the energy conditions locally at the photon sphere, we derive analytic and testable bounds on the shadow size, Lyapunov exponent, Lyapunov time, and photon-ring time delay that apply to all static, spherically symmetric black holes in General Relativity. Expressed equivalently in terms of either the spacetime metric functions or the local components of the stress-energy tensor, these bounds provide complementary tests of strong-field gravity that are independent of any specific black hole solution or matter model. They are directly applicable to current and forthcoming horizon-scale observations, constrain both admissible black hole spacetimes and their underlying matter sources, and establish the classical energy conditions as observational probes of strong-field gravity. Any observational violation of these bounds would signal either a violation of the classical energy conditions by the matter fields or a breakdown of General Relativity in the strong-field regime.

</details>


### [137] [Corrected thermodynamics and radiation predictions of modified black bounce compact objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11851)
*Shokhzod Jumaniyozov,Javlon Rayimbaev,Yassine Sekhmani,Satimbay Palvanov,Olmos Tursunboyev,Dilshod Karshiev*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了修正引力中Simpson-Visser正则化Schwarzschild黑洞所得规则致密天体（SV-MOG时空）的热力学性质与辐射特征。该时空通过MOG耦合参数与黑洞反弹参数平滑插值于规则黑洞、单向虫洞和可穿越虫洞之间。研究发现，黑洞和虫洞分支均存在二阶相变；量子引力修正导致小视界半径处熵偏离Bekenstein-Hawking面积律；增大MOG参数会扩大视界并增强辐射，而增大反弹参数则抑制视界并软化光谱轮廓，这些特征可作为区分SV-MOG致密天体与Schwarzschild及纯SV天体的观测信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在修正引力框架下，利用Simpson-Visser正则化方法构造规则致密天体（SV-MOG时空），旨在研究MOG耦合参数和黑洞反弹参数对时空结构、热力学性质及吸积盘辐射特征的影响，寻找可能区分这类天体与标准Schwarzschild黑洞及纯SV天体的观测特征。

Method: 1. 构建SV-MOG时空度规，其lapse函数包含MOG耦合参数和黑洞反弹参数；2. 推导Hawking温度和热容，分析相变行为；3. 引入含对数项的量子引力修正熵；4. 计算几何薄吸积盘的电磁通量、有效盘温度和光谱光度。

Result: 增大MOG参数扩大事件视界并增强辐射发射；增大反弹参数抑制视界并软化光谱轮廓；小视界半径处熵显著偏离Bekenstein-Hawking面积律；黑洞和虫洞分支均存在热容变号对应的二阶相变。

Conclusion: SV-MOG致密天体具有可观测的独特热力学和辐射特征，这些信号有望在观测上将其与标准Schwarzschild黑洞及纯SV天体区分开来。

Abstract: We study the thermodynamic properties and radiation characteristics of a regular compact object obtained by applying the Simpson-Visser regularisation to the Schwarzschild black hole in modified gravity. The resulting SV-MOG spacetime, whose lapse function involves both the MOG coupling parameter and the black bounce parameter, smoothly interpolates between a regular black hole, a one-way wormhole, and a traversable wormhole depending on the parameter. We derive the Hawking temperature and heat capacity for both the black hole and wormhole branches, identifying second-order phase transitions signaled by sign changes in the heat capacity. Quantum gravitational corrections to the entropy are incorporated via logarithmic terms parameterized by coefficients, and we show that deviations from the Bekenstein-Hawking area law become significant at small horizon radii. For the radiation sector, we compute the electromagnetic flux, effective disk temperature, and spectral luminosity of geometrically thin accretion disks surrounding both black hole and wormhole configurations. Our results demonstrate that increasing the modified parameter enlarges the event horizon and enhances the emission, while increasing the black bounce parameter suppresses the horizon and softens the spectral profile, providing observational signatures that may distinguish SV-MOG compact objects from their Schwarzschild and pure SV counterparts.

</details>


### [138] [Higher-derivative gravitational effective field theories are generically weakly hyperbolic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11879)
*Farid Thaalba,Fernando Abalos,Miguel Bezares*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明，在度规高阶导数有效引力理论中，只要特征速度不依赖于度规的导数，该理论本质上是弱双曲的，且这种弱双曲性是物理的，无法通过规范选择或添加约束消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分析度规高阶导数有效引力理论的初值问题，探究其双曲性性质，特别是强双曲性失效的结构性原因。

Method: 直接从特征方程中识别自旋-2物理扇区，无需引入降阶形式化，通过分析特征速度与度规导数无关的条件，证明该类理论包含弱双曲块。

Result: 任何运动方程中含有超过两个导数的度规理论，在其自旋-2扇区中必然包含一个弱双曲块，且这种弱双曲性是物理固有的，无法通过规范选择或添加约束来消除。

Conclusion: 该工作为高阶导数引力理论中强双曲性失效提供了结构性解释，表明这一障碍是物理性的而非规范选择问题。

Abstract: We analyse the initial-value problem of metric higher-derivative effective theories of gravity. We show that any such theory whose characteristic velocities are independent of derivatives of the metric is intrinsically weakly hyperbolic, independently of the gauge fixing. To show this, we identify the spin-$2$ physical sector directly from the characteristic equation; this can be done without introducing an order-reduced formulation, which greatly simplifies the computation. In this sector, every metric theory with more than two derivatives in the equations of motion contains a weakly hyperbolic block. Since this obstruction is physical, no choice of gauge or constraint addition can remove it, providing a structural explanation for the failure of strong hyperbolicity in this broad class of theories.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [139] [Knowledge Graphs Meet Graph Neural Networks: A Comprehensive Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09666)
*Chengcheng Sun,Jiayun Tian,Cheng Zhai,Zhixiao Wang,Yajie Song,Xiaobin Rui,Jian Zhang,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对GNN在知识图谱全技术管线中的应用缺乏系统综述的问题，提出了一个包含“KG技术管线”和“GNN视角”的双层分类框架，系统梳理了GNN在KG构建、嵌入、推理和应用中的方法，并总结了优势、局限和未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管GNN已成为知识图谱领域的重要范式，但尚无工作对整个KG技术管线中GNN方法进行系统性综述。作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出双层分类框架：(1) KG技术管线维度（KG构建、嵌入、推理、应用）；(2) GNN模型维度（GCN、GAT、HGNN等）。在此基础上，按任务特征分析GNN优势，并详细评述各类GNN模型在KG各环节的应用。

Result: 给出了系统性的分类与综述，总结了各GNN模型在KG不同任务中的优势与局限性。

Conclusion: 本文填补了GNN在KG全管线中系统性综述的空白，并指出了当前挑战和未来研究方向。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm in Knowledge Graphs (KGs) due to their intrinsic ability to model graph-structured data. However, there remains a lack of a systematic review about GNN-based methodologies across the entire knowledge graph technologies pipeline. To address this gap, we first propose a novel two-level taxonomy framework for GNN-based knowledge graph technologies: the KG technologies pipeline and GNN-based perspective. Specifically, the knowledge graph technologies pipeline covers knowledge graph construction, knowledge graph embedding, knowledge reasoning and knowledge graph applications. Meanwhile, the GNN-based perspective provides a new categorization of knowledge graph technologies with GNN models, such as GCN, GAT, and HGNN. Then, we analyze the advantages of GNN technology based on the characteristics of different tasks in the knowledge graph lifecycle. Furthermore, we detailed review various GNN-based models for knowledge graph following the proposed taxonomy, and summarize strengths and limitations. Finally, we discuss unresolved challenges and outline promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [140] [Position: Every Ground Truth is a Human Construction, not an Objective Truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09668)
*Charlotte Högberg,Ericka Johnson,Kiri L. Wagstaff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证了机器学习中的"地面真值"数据集并非中立客观的测量结果，而是由人类与技术共同构建的产物。作者呼吁学界关注这些隐性选择，承认参考数据集的偶然性与情境依赖性，并提出"情境可靠性"概念，强调明确模型真理主张的适用范围与局限性，以提升透明度、问责性和跨学科协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器学习社区普遍将地面真值数据集视为天然存在的客观基准，忽视了其构建过程中涉及的人为选择与技术中介。这种认知遮蔽导致模型评估的可靠性和适用范围被误解，因此需要揭示地面真值的社会技术建构性。

Method: 采用立场论文（position paper）的论证方法，通过理论分析阐述地面真值的情境依赖本质，提出"情境可靠性"（situated reliability）概念作为替代传统客观性框架的评估思路。

Result: 论证了地面真值的社会技术建构性（非中立性）、偶然性与非普遍性；提出了"情境可靠性"框架，强调明确模型真理主张的适用边界。

Conclusion: 关注地面真值的构建过程有助于提升透明度、问责性和跨学科协作，最终改善机器学习模型的可靠性与适用性评估。

Abstract: Ground truth datasets play a fundamental role as reference values in the training and evaluation of machine learning models. This position paper argues that ground truths are not neutral objective measurements that are naturally given, but instead that they are constructed by arrangements of humans and technologies. We argue that the ML community will benefit from articulating and discussing these often invisible or unreported choices and acknowledging that reference data sets are contingent, not universal. Focusing on the situated and context-dependent nature of ground truths can improve reliability by enabling a better informed perspective on where, when, and how the datasets, and the models they have shaped, can best be used. We argue for increasing `situated reliability' which includes articulating the limits and strengths of models and their truth claims. Finally, paying more attention to the construction of ground truths can support transparency, accountability, and interdisciplinary work.

</details>


### [141] [AuditWeave: A Tamper-Evident, Auditor-Navigable Evidence Layer for AI-Assisted and Data-Transformation Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09682)
*Vimal Nakrani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AuditWeave，一个轻量级Python库，通过追加式哈希链账本记录AI辅助决策与数据转换流程，实现端到端可追溯与防篡改验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具（如模型可观测性、漂移监控、治理报告）面向机器学习工程师而非审核者，无法满足监管领域中"将特定结论追溯至其支撑证据"并证明推理记录未被篡改的合规需求。

Method: 设计AuditWeave库：采用追加式哈希链账本结构，定义一套系统无关的事件词汇表，统一覆盖RAG流水线与表格/湖仓转换流程；通过链式验证检测任何事件的修改、重排、插入或删除。

Result: 每个事件仅需数十微秒即可完成完整性保证；在2000次随机试验中，针对四类篡改操作，验证算法成功检测所有注入的变异。

Conclusion: AuditWeave以极低开销实现了AI辅助决策流程的端到端可追溯性与防篡改保障，填补了现有工具在合规审核场景中的空白。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly used to assist consequential decisions in regulated domains such as auditing, finance, and healthcare. This creates a recurring obligation: an organization must be able to reconstruct, after the fact, which evidence informed a given conclusion, and to show that the record of that reasoning was not altered. Existing tools address related but distinct problems - model observability, drift monitoring, governance reporting - and are built for the machine-learning engineer operating a system, not the reviewer who must trace one specific conclusion back to its supporting evidence. We present AuditWeave, a lightweight Python library, with no runtime dependencies, that records the steps of AI-assisted and data-transformation workflows into a single append-only, hash-chained ledger. A small, system-agnostic event vocabulary spans both retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipelines and tabular/lakehouse transformations, so a conclusion that draws on both can be traced end-to-end through one record. Within a sealed ledger, any modification, reordering, insertion, or deletion of events is detectable through chain verification. We describe the design and evaluate recording overhead, scalability, and tamper-detection correctness on the reference implementation. The integrity guarantees cost tens of microseconds per event, and, as the hash-chain construction implies, verification flagged every injected mutation across four mutation classes over 2,000 randomized trials.

</details>


### [142] [Ablation, Statistical Inference, and Validation for KV-Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09683)
*Paolo D'Alberto,Ashish Siarasao,Elliott Delaye,Rajeev Patwari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了Turbo-Quant和SpectralQuant两种KV-cache压缩方法，通过统计验证方法评估了包括WHT旋转+Beta Lloyd-Max和QJL在内的非支配方案，发现基于特征基的方法在重尾数据上因协方差不稳定而失效，但在结构化场景中表现出色，且有效语义维度(d_eff)会根据校准预算而非真实数据秩自适应调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV-cache压缩方法缺乏系统性比较，且不同方法间的性能差异究竟源于算法本身还是实现差异尚不明确，需要一种能区分系统编解码差异与实现方差的统计验证方法论。

Method: 采用统计验证方法论，系统比较Turbo-Quant和SpectralQuant两种KV-cache压缩方法，评估非支配方案（包括WHT旋转+Beta Lloyd-Max和QJL），分离系统编解码差异与实现方差。

Result: 1) 基于特征基的方法在重尾数据上因协方差不稳定而失效；2) 在结构化场景中表现出色；3) 有效语义维度(d_eff)随校准预算调整，而非反映真实数据秩。

Conclusion: 不同KV-cache压缩方法在不同数据分布下表现迥异，选择方法需考虑数据特性；有效语义维度并非固定属性，而是依赖于校准预算的适应性指标。

Abstract: This study systematically compares Turbo-Quant and SpectralQuant KV-cache compression, evaluating non-dominated schemes, including WHT rotation with Beta Lloyd-Max and QJL, through a statistical validation methodology that separates systematic codec differences from implementation variance. Key findings reveal that while eigenbasis-based methods fail on heavy-tailed data due to covariance instability, they excel in structured regimes, with the effective semantic dimension ($d_{eff}$) adapting to calibration budgets rather than true data rank. (this is an abstract of the abstract thank you )

</details>


### [143] [SciML in the Wild: A Diagnostic Study of When Structural Priors Help and When They Hurt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09684)
*Vrishank Sai Anand,Prathamesh Dinesh Joshi,Raj Abhijit Dandekar,Rajat Dandekar,Sreedath Panat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在宏观经济预测这一低频率、稀疏数据的压力测试场景下，评估了五种模型（ARIMA、LSTM、NODE、PINN、UDE）在23个国家的表现，发现没有模型能稳定取得强预测性能，但约束较少的模型（ARIMA、NODE）一致优于强约束先验模型（PINN、UDE）。研究指出，当结构性先验与数据生成过程不匹配时，先验知识会退化为“误导正则化器”，并识别了先验错配、制度突变、结构断裂和优化不稳定等失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SciML方法（如NODE、PINN、UDE）依赖可靠的结构先验来提升建模效果，但现实场景中先验可能不可靠。本文旨在探究当结构先验与真实数据生成过程不匹配时，SciML方法是否仍然有效，并以宏观经济预测这一高难度领域作为压力测试。

Method: 使用23个国家的稀疏年度宏观经济数据，采用多个时间分割和五种随机种子，系统评估ARIMA（统计基线）、LSTM（纯数据驱动）、NODE（弱先验）、PINN（强先验）和UDE（混合先验）五种模型家族的预测性能。

Result: 所有模型均未取得一致的强预测性能，但存在明确的相对排序：约束较少的模型（ARIMA、NODE）一贯优于强约束先验模型（PINN、UDE）。约束更强的模型反而表现更差，结构先验在错配时成为“误导正则化器”。

Conclusion: SciML方法不应盲目假设“更多结构更好”，而应先通过实证检验结构先验是否与数据生成过程吻合。本文的诊断结果为SciML在低频率、易发生结构突变的真实世界应用提供了警示，建议实践者在施加先验前先验证其有效性。

Abstract: Scientific Machine Learning (SciML) methods such as Neural Ordinary Differential Equations (NODEs), Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs), and Universal Differential Equations (UDEs) are most effective when structural priors reflect reliable governing dynamics. We ask what happens when this assumption is violated. Using macroeconomic forecasting as a stress-test domain, we evaluate five model families, ARIMA, LSTM, NODE, PINN, and UDE, across 23 countries using sparse annual data, multiple temporal splits, and five random seeds. Our results show that none of the evaluated models achieve consistently strong forecasting performance, highlighting the difficulty of low-frequency macroeconomic prediction. However, a clear relative hierarchy emerges: less-constrained models, particularly ARIMA and NODE, consistently outperform more-constrained heuristic-prior models such as PINN and UDE. Rather than treating this as a rejection of SciML, we interpret it as a diagnostic result: structural priors can act as misregularizers when they do not match the data-generating process. We identify failure modes including prior misalignment, regime shifts, structural breaks, and optimization instability, and argue that SciML practitioners should test whether structure helps before assuming that more structure is beneficial.

</details>


### [144] [MawForge: Memory-Bounded Expert Materialization for Local Mixture-of-Experts Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09686)
*Craig Opie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MawForge提出了一种在统一内存设备上本地运行MoE语言模型的新方法：将完整模型存储在磁盘中，仅常驻公共张量，并按需将路由专家张量加载到有限执行缓存中。研究发现该方法作为有界执行机制和测量框架有效，但作为缓存最大化策略效果不佳。性能取决于专家复用率、驻留内存占用、KV缓存大小、量化精度、路由局部性和macOS内存压力之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决稀疏MoE语言模型在本地推理时因完整模型、KV缓存、运行时缓冲区和操作系统开销过大，难以适应有限快速内存的问题，探索在受约束的统一内存机器上实现本地MoE服务实际可行的系统方案。

Method: 将完整MoE模型存储在磁盘上，常驻公共张量（如嵌入、注意力层等），将路由专家张物按需从磁盘加载到有界执行缓存中，通过控制缓存大小和专家替换策略来管理内存压力。

Result: MawForge作为有界执行机制和测量框架有效，但作为缓存最大化策略效果不佳。系统性能高度依赖于专家复用率、驻留内存占用、KV缓存大小、量化精度、路由局部性和macOS内存压力之间复杂的平衡关系。

Conclusion: 在统一内存设备上本地运行MoE模型存在根本性的权衡，单纯追求缓存最大化并非最优策略，需要更精细地平衡多因素才能实现实际可用的本地MoE推理系统。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models separate total parameter count from per-token active computation, but local inference systems often still require the full model, key-value cache, runtime buffers, and operatingsystem headroom to fit in fast memory. MawForge tests a different systems hypothesis: local MoE serving can be made practical on constrained unified-memory machines by storing the full model on disk, keeping common tensors resident, and materializing routed expert tensors into a bounded execution cache on demand.
