Recent approaches use latency predictors to replace costly HIL feedback, but challenges persist: (1) platform-specific predictors often require tens of thousands of samples, and (2) inaccurate predictions can mislead the NAS process. To address this, we introduce HiFi-LLP, a high-fidelity, low-cost latency predictor based on graph attention networks, augmented with a confidence metric. HiFi-LLP outperforms prior platform-specific predictors by up to 9 percentage points (p.p.) in the 10% accuracy bound and achieves a Spearman's rank correlation of up to 0.996 across six devices in the LatBench dataset. We further propose a hybrid NAS framework that routes low-confidence predictions to HIL, achieving up to 8.6$\times$ speedup compared to typical NAS while maintaining a competitive Pareto front.

</details>


### [301] [From Global to Factor-Wise Expert Composition in Discrete Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11758)
*Haozhe Huang,Yudong Xu,Abhijoy Mandal,Alán Aspuru-Guzik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FactorDiff框架，通过因子级分解和动态路由机制实现扩散模型组合生成，在ARC-AGI基准上优于全局标量加权方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于时间依赖混合权重的扩散模型组合方法在逐样本基础上工作，将每个生成状态视为整体，忽视了不同专家在空间或功能上的专业化差异，限制了组合生成的表达能力。

Method: 提出FactorDiff框架，将样本分解为更小的因子（如空间/像素级），采样过程中动态地将每个因子路由到最相关的专家模型。

Result: 在ARC-AGI基准上，简单的因子特定路由方法在需要逻辑一致性和空间解耦的任务上持续优于复杂的全局标量加权方案。

Conclusion: 因子级的分解与动态路由能有效提升扩散模型组合生成的性能，特别是在需要空间解耦和逻辑一致性的复杂推理任务中。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models offer a powerful framework for solving complex reasoning tasks, particularly through compositional generation, which combines multiple pre-trained experts to generalize beyond their individual training data. Recent theoretical corrections introduce time-dependent mixing weights to better align composed diffusion dynamics with the intended target. However, these methods are fundamentally limited by working on a per-sample basis, treating each generated state monolithically and ignoring the potential spatial or functional specializations of different experts. In this work, we address this limitation by proposing FactorDiff - a factor-wise composition framework for diffusion models. We posit that samples can be further decomposed into smaller factors, and propose a sampling process that dynamically routes each factor to the most relevant expert. We instantiate this framework with spatial/pixel-level compositions and validate it on the ARC-AGI benchmark, demonstrating that simple factor-specific routing consistently outperforms complex global scalar weighting schemes on tasks that require logical consistency and spatial disentanglement.

</details>


### [302] [From Expressivity to Sample Complexity: Narrow Teachers for Transformers via C-RASP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11760)
*Michael Rizvi-Martel,Satwik Bhattamishra,Guillaume Rabusseau,Michael Hahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出针对Transformer模型学习C-RASP构造的样本复杂度界限，填补了现有理论工作在可学习性分析方面的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论工作主要关注Transformer的表达能力（通过手工构造权重或计算复杂性论证哪些任务在假设类中），但很少研究这些解的可学习性（样本复杂度），本文旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: 受近期损失景观分析工作的启发，提出用于学习C-RASP构造的Transformer的初步样本复杂度界限。

Result: 得到了针对C-RASP构造的Transformer学习的样本复杂度界限。

Conclusion: 为理解Transformer模型在何种样本条件下能够学习到特定任务提供了理论基础，推进了对LLM可学习性的理解。

Abstract: A theoretical understanding of Transformers is crucial to better understand the capacities and limitations of large language models (LLMs). There is much work analyzing the expressivity of attention-based models. By proposing handcrafted weights or using computational complexity arguments, a large amount of past theoretical works have sought to characterize which tasks are and which are not in the hypothesis class of Transformer models. However, little work investigates the learnability of such solutions. In this work, we make progress towards this goal. Inspired by recent loss landscape analysis work, we propose preliminary sample complexity bounds for learning C-RASP constructions with Transformers.

</details>


### [303] [An Exact Instrument for State Usage in Selective State-Space Models, and the Input-Driven Migration It Reveals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11796)
*Raktim Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种精确分析选择性状态空间模型（如Mamba）中模态使用情况的工具，发现模型会随输入动态重新分配状态空间，其中输入相关的写入映射B_t是主要驱动因素，并据此实现了输入调度的模态剪枝，在半状态预算下匹配未剪枝模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的选择性状态空间模型（如Mamba）通过一种学习到的选择机制来控制信息路由，但缺乏精确的工具来测量训练后的模型如何使用这些模态，因此需要一种仪器来量化每个模态的贡献以及模态子集剪枝的影响。

Method: 利用状态矩阵的对角化性质，将每个通道的输出精确分解为每个模态的贡献；构建每（层、通道、窗口）的Gram张量，用于计算任意模态子集丢弃后的精确输出误差；通过冻结信号的反事实归因分析，分离选择机制中B_t和时滞步长对模态迁移的贡献。

Result: 在Mamba-1系列（130M-2.8B）、Falcon-Mamba 7B和Mamba-2上验证，仪器预测部署剪枝误差的中位数相对偏差为5×10^{-7}；发现模型随输入动态重新分配状态空间，最受影响的层中每个输入的自适应模态集可将输出误差减半；输入调度的模态剪枝在所有规模上优于静态、Hankel和层级自适应剪枝，在半状态预算下匹配未剪枝模型。

Conclusion: 训练好的Mamba模型会根据输入上下文动态迁移信号所承载的模态，这种迁移主要由输入相关的写入映射B_t驱动，而非通常被认为与选择性相关的时滞步长；输入调度的模态剪枝是有效的，但本文未声明实际的部署计算或内存节省。

Abstract: Selective state-space models such as Mamba route information through a bank of first-order modes whose input coupling is set by a learned selection mechanism. We give an exact instrument for measuring how a trained model uses these modes. Because the state matrix is diagonal, each channel's output decomposes exactly into per-mode contributions, and a per-(layer, channel, window) Gram tensor yields the exact output error of dropping any subset of modes, offline, at any budget. Validated against the reference implementation to a relative error of $2.3\times10^{-7}$ on the Mamba-1 family where it is exact, the instrument predicts a layer's deployed pruning error to a median relative deviation of $5\times10^{-7}$ over $4{,}464$ configurations, its floor set by the reconstruction. Applying the instrument across the Mamba-1 family (130M--2.8B), the deployed 7B Falcon-Mamba, and Mamba-2, we find that trained models re-allocate their state space with the input: which modes carry the signal migrates across contexts, and at the most affected layers a per-input oracle roughly halves the output error of a fixed mode set. Frozen-signal counterfactuals attribute the migration primarily to the input-dependent write map $B_t$; the timestep usually identified with selectivity carries almost none of it. Input-scheduled mode pruning on this measurement outperforms static, Hankel-based, and layer-adaptive rankings at every scale from 130M to the deployed 7B Falcon-Mamba, and at half the state budget it matches the unpruned model. Because the scheduler reads each window's mode usage from a first pass, this demonstrates realizable headroom; we claim no deployed compute or memory saving.

</details>


### [304] [Relaxing Faithfulness with Intervention-Only Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11816)
*Bijan Mazaheri,Jiaqi Zhang,Caroline Uhler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在因果发现中，硬干预包含被条件独立性测试忽略的因果链接信息，通过“干预即时忠实性”假设（允许路径抵消），可在非忠实性条件下非参数识别因果结构，主张将干预信息置于条件独立性测试之上，并定义了干预受限时的等价类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因果发现算法依赖忠实性假设（因果关联必存在统计依赖），但许多自然系统中存在缓冲和稳定通路，这些通路相互抵消导致违反忠实性，使算法错误移除因果依赖关系。本文旨在解决这一局限性。

Method: 提出“干预即时忠实性”假设（允许通路抵消的温和条件），证明在该假设下结合硬干预可非参数识别因果结构；同时定义了识别条件不满足时的等价类。

Result: 证明了在干预即时忠实性假设下，硬干预信息足以非参数识别因果结构，即使存在通路抵消（违反传统忠实性）；并给出了干预范围受限时的等价类描述。

Conclusion: 干预信息应作为因果结构发现的主要依据，优先于条件独立性测试；本文结果为非忠实性场景下的因果发现提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Causal discovery algorithms learn a network that describes the causal dependencies among random variables. A common workflow involves first utilizing conditional independence properties on observational data to determine partially directed causal relationships, then applying interventions to orient the unknown causal directions. A critical assumption for the first step is faithfulness: a requirement that causally linked variables exhibit statistical dependence. Many natural systems include buffering and stabilizing pathways that cancel out to achieve systemic robustness. This cancellation of pathways violates faithfulness, leading causal discovery algorithms to incorrectly remove causal dependencies. In this paper, we argue that hard interventions contain information about the presence/absence of causal linkage that is overlooked in the first stage of structure discovery. We show that a mild assumption -- called intervention-immediacy faithfulness -- that allows cancellations, is sufficient to nonparametrically identify causal structures with hard interventions. These results position interventions as the primary carriers of information about causal structure, which should take precedence over conditional independence testing. To flip the paradigm, we also specify equivalence classes when the identification criteria are not met due to limitations in the scope of interventions.

</details>


### [305] [Transformer-Guided Swarm Intelligence for Frugal Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11826)
*Romain Amigon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级、低成本的神经架构搜索框架，结合强化学习Transformer与人工蜂群算法，在消费级GPU上仅用3小时即可搜索出参数高效的高精度模型（CIFAR-10上84.85%准确率，仅17.4万参数；欺诈检测F1为0.71，仅4600参数），有效降低了NAS的计算门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统NAS计算成本极高（需数千GPU天），难以在消费级硬件上普及。本文旨在设计一种廉价的、能在普通GPU上运行的NAS框架，使模型架构设计民主化。

Method: 提出一种节俭的模因式NAS框架，结合全局宏搜索（基于强化学习训练的自回归Transformer控制器）与局部微探索（人工蜂群算法）；引入动态熵机制防止RL阶段过早收敛；通过算法惩罚网络深度抑制模型臃肿。

Result: 在NVIDIA RTX 3060上仅3小时完成搜索：CIFAR-10达84.85%准确率、约17.4万参数（远小于ResNet-20等基线）；信用卡欺诈检测F1-Score达0.71、约4600参数。

Conclusion: 该框架能够产出定制化、可访问、参数高效的深度学习模型，适用于边缘部署，有效解决了元启发式方法的冷启动问题。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) has automated the design of deep learning models but traditionally requires massive computational resources, often measured in thousands of GPU-days. In this paper, we propose a frugal and memetic NAS framework designed to democratize architecture design on consumer-grade hardware. Our approach combines the global macro-search capabilities of an autoregressive Transformer controller, trained via Reinforcement Learning (RL), with the local micro-exploitation of an Artificial Bee Colony (ABC) algorithm. To prevent premature convergence during the RL phase, we introduce a dynamic entropy mechanism that forces topological exploration upon detection of performance stagnation. Evaluated on a standard GPU (NVIDIA RTX 3060), our hybrid method effectively resolves the "cold-start" problem inherent in metaheuristics. By algorithmically penalizing network depth, our framework actively mitigates model bloat: on the CIFAR-10 dataset, it discovers an efficient architecture reaching 84.85% accuracy with only $\sim$174,000 parameters (significantly smaller than standard baselines like ResNet-20) in 3 hours of search time. Furthermore, we demonstrate the framework's flexibility by applying it to credit card fraud detection, directly optimizing the F1-Score on highly imbalanced tabular data to reach a F1-Score of 0.71 with a compact network of $\sim$4,600 parameters. These results suggest that our approach can yield tailored, accessible, and highly parameter-efficient deep learning models suitable for edge deployment.

</details>


### [306] [Inside the Unfair Judge: A Mechanistic Interpretability Account of LLM-as-Judge Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11871)
*Zixiang Xu,Sixian Li,Huaxing Liu,Xiang Wang,Shuai Li,Zirui Song,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM作为评判者时的评分偏差不仅存在于输入-输出层面，还可在隐藏状态中表示为低维、类型特定的几何子空间：该子空间随层数加深而锐化，沿该方向进行因果控制可双向调节评分，且线性投影到该方向可预测未见过的基准上的评判失败，优于基于文本的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅从输入-输出层面分析LLM评判偏差，未探索隐藏状态中的表示级解释，缺乏对偏差内在机制的理解和更有效的操作手段。

Method: 在7个评判模型、7种偏差类型、9个基准上，通过几何分析识别偏差子空间，使用因果干预（隐藏状态操控）验证方向性，并用线性投影预测模型在未见基准上的失效情况。

Result: 偏差输入激活沿低维、类型特定子空间位移，该子空间随深度锐化；沿该方向操控可双向调节评分；线性投影到该方向可大幅优于文本方法预测模型失败。

Conclusion: 将偏差视为激活几何而非输入-输出噪声，统一了几何结构、因果控制和操作预测三个层面的发现，提供了互补且操作上更优的视角。

Abstract: Existing studies of LLM-as-judge scoring bias work predominantly at the input-output level: they perturb inputs, measure score deltas, and propose prompt-level mitigations. We argue that the same biases admit a representation-level account in the judge's hidden state, complementary to the input-output view and operationally useful in ways it does not afford. We report three findings, across seven judges, seven bias types, and nine benchmarks. Geometry: baseline judging inputs occupy a tight activation manifold while biased inputs are displaced along a low-dimensional, type-specific subspace that sharpens with depth and is recovered consistently by three families of estimators. Causal control: steering hidden states along this subspace drives scoring in both directions, forward shifts reproducing biased scoring on clean inputs and reverse shifts restoring baseline scoring on biased ones, while matched-norm random directions produce shifts an order of magnitude smaller. Operational: a simple linear projection onto the same bias-direction features anticipates judge failures on three entirely unseen benchmarks, substantially outperforming text-based alternatives. Reading bias as activation geometry, rather than as input-output noise, unifies geometric structure, causal control, and operational prediction within a single framework. The project page is available at https://xzx34.github.io/unfair-judge/

</details>


### [307] [Invariant Learning Dynamics of Transformers in Inductive Reasoning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11875)
*Tiberiu Musat,Tiago Pimentel,Nicholas Zucchet,Thomas Hofmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个理论框架，解释Transformer语言模型中归纳推理能力的涌现机制。通过定义一类统一的归纳任务（涵盖上下文n-gram和多跳推理等），证明注意力模型的训练动力学可被限制在高度可解释的低维不变流形上，从而将学习动态简化为少量可解释坐标。利用该框架，揭示了数据统计如何控制上下文学习与权重学习的竞争、随机初始化如何决定胜出的电路，并展示了如何自动检测训练模型中学到的电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对Transformer学习动力学的研究大多局限于特定任务，缺乏统一的理论视角。本文旨在建立一个广义的归纳任务框架，系统性地解释Transformer如何涌现出归纳推理能力，并揭示学习动态背后的低维机制。

Method: 提出一类统一的归纳任务（包括上下文n-gram与多跳推理），通过数学证明表明注意力模型的训练动力学可被限制在低维不变流形上。在此流形上，学习动态由少量可解释坐标而非数百万参数描述，从而简化理论和实证分析。

Result: 成功刻画了数据统计如何调控上下文学习与权重学习的竞争关系；揭示了随机初始化如何影响多解情况下的电路选择；证明了基于流形的坐标框架可用于自动检测训练模型中学到的电路。

Conclusion: 将电路形成视为低维动力学现象，为建立Transformer学习的预测性理论迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: We present a theoretical framework to explain the emergence of inductive reasoning abilities in Transformer language models. While previous works on Transformer learning dynamics have so far been mostly tied to specific tasks, we study a generalized class of inductive tasks that unifies several synthetic tasks known in the literature, including in-context n-grams and multi-hop reasoning. In this class, we theoretically prove that the training dynamics of attention models can be confined to a highly interpretable, low-dimensional invariant manifold. On this manifold, the learning dynamics are captured by a handful of interpretable coordinates rather than millions of parameters, making both theoretical and empirical analysis more tractable. Using this framework, we characterize how data statistics govern the competition between in-context and in-weights learning, we study how random initializations determine the `winning' circuit when multiple solutions are possible, and we demonstrate that the coordinate frame associated with the manifold can be used to automatically detect which circuits have been learned in trained models. By casting circuit formation as a low-dimensional dynamical phenomenon, we take a step toward a predictive theory of how Transformers learn.

</details>


### [308] [Requential Coding: Pushing the Limits of Model Compression with Self-Generated Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11883)
*Shikai Qiu,Marc Finzi,Yujia Zheng,Kun Zhang,Andrew Gordon Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"requential coding"方法，通过教师模型从学生自身分布中选择训练样本来压缩模型，码长独立于参数量和数据熵，比传统方法短数个数量级，在PAC-Bayes泛化界、过拟合预测和数据可学习信息隔离等方面取得突破性成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型压缩方法（如量化）码长随参数量增长，忽视了参数实际存储的信息量；预序编码（prequential coding）虽绕过参数量问题，但码长受数据熵影响，当数据熵高时码长仍然很大。需要一种与参数量和数据熵均无关的压缩方法。

Method: 提出requential coding框架：教师模型从学生自身分布中选取训练样本，学生仅记录这些选择结果。只有在教师和学生不一致的地方才产生比特开销，从而使得码长独立于参数量和数据熵。

Result: 码长比预序编码短数个数量级，且优势随规模增长；固定损失下，更大模型和集成模型压缩后尺寸更小（尽管参数量更多）；在十亿参数LLM上取得SOTA PAC-Bayes泛化保证；泛化界在计算最优区间随规模收紧；能预测多epoch训练下的过拟合；能隔离数据中的可学习信息与随机内容，发现低熵文本比高熵图像包含更多可学习结构。

Conclusion: Requential coding是一种全新的、与参数量和无关且与数据熵均无关的模型压缩范式，揭示了传统方法无法触及的规律性，为理解大规模模型的泛化能力和可压缩性提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Compression is fundamental to intelligence. A model that can represent its training data as a short code has discovered regularities that enable generalization. Large neural networks may learn functions far simpler than their parameter counts suggest, but it is challenging to construct codes that realize this simplicity. Parameter-based methods such as quantization produce code lengths that scale with model size, insensitive to how much information the parameters store. Prequential coding bypasses this issue by compressing the training trajectory, but codes the exact data sequence regardless of how much the model learns, yielding large codes when the data has high entropy. We introduce requential coding, where a teacher model selects training samples drawn from the student's own distribution. The student's code records only these selections, which cost bits only where teacher and student disagree. The resulting code length is independent of parameter count and data entropy, and often orders of magnitude shorter than the prequential counterpart, with an advantage that grows with scale. This compression sheds light on phenomena inaccessible to prior compressors. Holding loss fixed, larger models and ensembles compress to much smaller sizes despite more parameters. Plugged into a PAC-Bayes bound, the requential code yields state-of-the-art generalization guarantees for billion-parameter LLMs, outperforming bounds built on aggressive post-training quantization even granted zero error. The bound tightens with scale in the compute-optimal regime, as models become increasingly compressible relative to dataset size. The same code predicts that models gradually overfit when trained for multiple epochs. It also isolates the learnable information in a dataset from its unpredictable, random content, revealing that lower-entropy text holds far more learnable structure than higher-entropy image data.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [309] [Stochastic simulations of nonlinear reaction-diffusion equations using an exponential integrator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10065)
*Elliot J. Carr*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于指数积分器的新方法，用于从非线性反应扩散方程生成随机模拟，该方法通过矩阵函数条目定义有效概率，消除了传统时间离散化方案对时间步长的严格限制，并在多孔-Fisher类型模型中验证了其准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法在从确定性反应扩散方程生成随机模拟时，常用的时间离散化方案对时间步长有严格条件限制，这限制了其适用性和效率。本文旨在提出一种更灵活的方案，能够在更宽松的时间步长条件下生成有效的随机模拟。

Method: 将空间和时间离散化后的方程组系数解释为控制运动和反应事件的概率，并采用指数积分器进行时间离散化，通过适当的矩阵函数条目定义有效概率，避免了传统方案对时间步长的严格约束。

Result: 在一维和二维多孔-Fisher类型模型的多个测试问题上的仿真结果验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的基于指数积分器的随机模拟方法成功克服了传统时间离散化方案的时间步长限制，为非线性反应扩散方程的随机模拟提供了更灵活、更鲁棒的方案。

Abstract: Stochastic simulations can be generated from deterministic reaction-diffusion equations by discretising in space and time and interpreting coefficients in the resulting system of discretised equations as probabilities governing movement and reaction events. In this paper, we present a novel variant of this approach for nonlinear reaction-diffusion equations that employs an exponential integrator when discretising in time. The proposed method yields valid probabilities, defined by the entries of appropriate matrix functions, without the strict conditions on the time step required by a commonly-employed time discretisation scheme. Simulation results presented for one and two dimensional Porous-Fisher type models demonstrate the veracity of the method across several test problems.

</details>


### [310] [Functional Expansion Tallies of Matrix Operators for Prediction for Integrated Autocorrelation Time in Batch Monte Carlo: an Analytic 2D Scattering Chain Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10758)
*Jilang Miao,Guillaume L. Giudicelli,William Reed Kendrick*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了函数展开计数法作为降阶基表示，用于预测蒙特卡罗输运中的循环间相关性。通过二维各向同性散射链基准问题，比较了传统离散单元马尔可夫链估计器与基于基函数乘积的伽辽金降阶模型。降阶模型无需构建大型离散转移矩阵即可估计积分自相关时间。余弦基收敛迅速，多项式基随阶数增加系统收敛。与离散分箱相比，降阶基方法在相当或更低的求解成本下实现了更低偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒙特卡罗临界计算中，预测循环间相关性对于不确定性量化和方差缩减至关重要，但传统离散方法需要构建高维转移矩阵，计算成本高昂。本文旨在探索函数展开计数法作为降阶基表示是否能更高效地实现相关性预测。

Method: 使用二维各向同性散射链基准问题（带反射边界），构建两种方法：1）传统离散单元马尔可夫链估计器；2）伽辽金降阶模型，直接基于蒙特卡罗计数得到的基函数乘积构建。采用余弦基和多项式基两种函数展开基底。

Result: 余弦基快速收敛到精确结果；多项式基随阶数增加系统收敛；与离散分箱相比，降阶基方法在相当或更低求解成本下实现了更低偏差。

Conclusion: 函数展开计数法为蒙特卡罗临界计算中的相关性预测、不确定性量化及未来方差缩减方法提供了一种高效路径。

Abstract: We investigate functional expansion tallies as a reduced-basis representation for predicting inter-cycle correlations in Monte Carlo transport. Using an analytic two-dimensional isotropic scattering-chain benchmark with reflective boundaries, we compare a conventional discrete-cell Markov-chain estimator with a Galerkin reduced-order model built directly from Monte Carlo tallies of basis-function products. The reduced model estimates integrated autocorrelation time without first constructing a large discrete transition matrix. For the benchmark problem, the cosine basis converges rapidly to the exact result, while polynomial bases show systematic convergence with increasing order. Compared with discrete binning, the reduced-basis approach achieves lower bias at comparable or lower solve cost, suggesting that functional-expansion representations can provide an efficient path toward correlation prediction, uncertainty quantification, and future variance-reduction methods in Monte Carlo criticality calculations.

</details>


### [311] [Understanding Chemical Short-Range Order in CoNiV via Mode Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10775)
*Jilang Miao,Miaomiao Jin*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 利用机器学习势的分子动力学模拟，分析了等原子fcc NiCoV合金中的化学短程序，发现径向分布函数收敛后仍存在缓慢的化学有序化过程，壳层分辨的键统计量能更有效地追踪短程序演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的径向分布函数（RDF）在描述多组分合金的化学短程序（SRO）时可能无法捕捉到缓慢的化学弛豫过程，需要更精细的统计量来刻画合金中不同元素的局域化学有序化行为。

Method: 使用机器学习势生成分子动力学快照，计算壳层分辨的Warren-Cowley参数和键概率；采用延迟Jensen-Shannon诊断分析松弛速率差异；利用主成分分析提取每副本中心键概率的集体模式。

Result: 第一壳层中V-V规避、第二壳层中V-V富集（符合L1₂型局域有序倾向），第三壳层响应较弱；键统计量的松弛速度明显慢于RDF；PCA识别出三个集体模式：V子晶格有序化振幅、Ni-Co重新分布模式、Co-V交换模式。

Conclusion: 标量RDF的收敛可能掩盖缓慢的化学弛豫，壳层分辨的键统计量为追踪多组分合金中SRO的发展提供了一种紧凑而有效的途径。

Abstract: We analyze chemical short-range order in equiatomic fcc NiCoV using molecular-dynamics snapshots generated with a machine-learned interatomic potential. Radial distribution functions identify stable coordination shells, while shell-resolved Warren-Cowley parameters and bond probabilities reveal continued chemical ordering after the radial structure has largely converged. The dominant signal is V-V avoidance in the first shell and V-V enrichment in the second shell, consistent with an L1$_2$-like local ordering tendency, while the third-shell response remains weak. Lagged Jensen-Shannon diagnostics show that bond statistics relax more slowly than the RDF. Principal component analysis of per-replica-centered bond probabilities resolves three collective modes: a V-sublattice ordering amplitude, a Ni-Co redistribution mode, and a Co-V exchange-like mode. These results show that scalar RDF convergence can miss slow chemical relaxation, and that shell-resolved bond statistics provide a compact route for tracking SRO development in multicomponent alloys.

</details>


### [312] [Structure-preserving variational neural fields: Uncertainty-quantified reduced-order modeling of nonlinear conservation laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10965)
*Aviral Prakash,Marc L. Klasky*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种变分潜在神经场框架，将高斯过程启发的替代模型与守恒律保持相结合，同时提供不确定性量化与精确守恒律保持。该框架包含三种变体（IRS-UQ、PI-IRS-UQ、ECLEIRS-UQ），并通过1-D平流、2-D欧拉和2-D浅水方程数值实验验证了其在稀疏噪声训练数据下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜动力学模型在加速参数化物理系统仿真时存在两个局限性：无法提供预测的不确定性估计，且不能保证遵守底层守恒律。此前工作分别解决了这两方面挑战，但缺乏一个统一框架来同时实现不确定性量化与精确守恒律保持。

Method: 开发了一个变分潜在神经场框架，集成高斯过程启发的替代模型以估计预测置信度。提出三种变体：无约束的IRS-UQ、基于物理信息的PI-IRS-UQ、以及通过时空散度自由表示将解动态嵌入守恒律流形实现精确守恒结构保持的ECLEIRS-UQ。

Result: 数值实验表明所提方法能提供准确预测和不确定性估计，对稀疏噪声训练数据具有鲁棒性。三种方法的比较显示，守恒结构保持的潜在表示在保持预测精度和不确定性量化能力的同时，提升了对退化训练数据的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出的统一框架有效解决了潜动力学模型中不确定性估计与守恒律保持的双重挑战，为参数化物理系统的可信仿真提供了一种新范式。

Abstract: Reduced-order models, such as latent dynamics models, are becoming mainstream for accelerating simulations for parameterized physical systems governed by nonlinear conservation laws. However, most existing latent dynamics frameworks suffer from two important limitations: they do not provide uncertainty estimates for model predictions, and they do not guarantee adherence to the underlying conservation laws. While these challenges have been addressed separately in prior work, a unified framework that simultaneously provides uncertainty quantification and exact conservation-law preservation remains largely unexplored. In this work, we develop a variational latent neural field framework that integrates Gaussian process-inspired surrogates, enabling estimation of predictive confidence for both in-distribution and out-of-distribution parameter regimes. Three variants of the framework are considered: IRS-UQ, PI-IRS-UQ, and ECLEIRS-UQ, corresponding to unconstrained, physics-informed, and conservation-structure-preserving formulations, respectively. Exact conservation-structure preservation is achieved by embedding the solution dynamics within a conservation-law manifold through a space-time divergence-free representation of the solution-flux field. We demonstrate the applicability of the framework through three numerical experiments: 1) 1-D advection, 2) 2-D Euler and 3) 2-D shallow water equations in parameterized settings. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed approach provides accurate predictions together with uncertainty estimates, while remaining robust to sparse and noisy training data. Comparisons between the proposed three approaches show that conservation-structure preserving latent representations improve robustness to degraded training data while maintaining competitive predictive accuracy and uncertainty quantification capability.

</details>


### [313] [Compact and Stable Representation of Real-Frequency Spectral Functions for Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11190)
*Xinyang Dong,Ido Zemach,Lei Zhang,Guy Cohen,Emanuel Gull*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Cayley映射三角矩的紧致稳定表示方法，用于实频率格林函数、杂化函数和自能的机器学习，避免了密集频率网格和Matsubara解析延拓的问题，并在DMFT等多种模型中验证了其准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习处理量子多体问题中实频率格林函数时，稠密频率网格效率低下，而Matsubara方法涉及病态的解析延拓，缺乏既紧致又稳定的表示方法。

Method: 构造基于Cayley映射的三角矩表示（包含Jacobian），保持谱权重归一化，将矩序列与正定矩阵值谱测度关联，并通过ESPRIT实现极点表示。采用带有FiLM条件的图注意力神经网络进行学习。

Result: 在单轨道DMFT、反铁磁DMFT和双轨道杂质模型上，该方法在精度上达到或超越直接频域学习，能可靠重现密度和交错磁化强度，通过Dyson方程反演稳定重建自能，准确恢复含轨道混合的矩阵值谱。

Conclusion: 该矩表示方法为实频率格林函数的机器学习提供了固定维度的学习目标，可直接施加归一化和正定性等物理约束，是频域学习的高效替代方案。

Abstract: We introduce a compact and stable moment representation for real-frequency Green's functions, hybridization functions, and self-energies for machine-learning applications, avoiding the inefficiency of dense frequency grids as well as the ill-posed analytic continuation of Matsubara approaches. The representation is constructed from Cayley-mapped trigonometric moments with the Jacobian included, which preserve spectral-weight normalization, tie the moment sequence to a positive matrix-valued spectral measure, and admit a systematic route to a pole representation via ESPRIT. This provides a fixed-dimensional learning target in which physical constraints such as normalization and positivity can be imposed directly. Using a graph-attention neural network with FiLM conditioning, we benchmark the representation on single-orbital DMFT, antiferromagnetic DMFT, and a two-orbital impurity model. The results demonstrate accuracy matching or exceeding that of direct frequency-domain learning, reliable reproduction of the density and staggered magnetization, stable self-energy reconstruction through Dyson equation inversion, and accurate recovery of matrix-valued spectra with orbital mixing.

</details>


### [314] [Uncertainty-Aware Structure-Property Mapping of Spinodoid Metamaterials via Heteroscedastic Gaussian Process Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11209)
*Minwoo Park,Junseo Park,Mingyu Lee,Hugon Lee,Hanbin Cho,Ikjin Lee,Seunghwa Ryu*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种不确定性感知的结构-性能映射框架，利用异方差高斯过程回归从稀疏数据中推断自旋体超材料的随机属性分布，并证明异方差RBDO才能满足可靠性目标，揭示了确定性优化和同方差方法在考虑形态变异时的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法将自旋体超材料的锥角描述符视为确定性输入，忽略了高斯随机场拓扑生成中的随机性——相同锥角描述符可产生不同形态实现和属性散布。需要建立不确定性感知的映射框架。

Method: 采用异方差高斯过程回归（GPR），从每个设计点仅有一个实现点的稀疏数据中推断输入相关的预测不确定性，无需在每个设计点收集经验方差标签。

Result: 刚度散布在不同张量分量间存在差异，取决于各分量机械活跃方向；产生相同平均刚度的参数集可能携带不同的偶然不确定性。确定性最优解在考虑形态诱导变异时高度易违反约束，同方差RBDO无法达到可靠性目标，仅异方差RBDO满足可靠性要求。

Conclusion: 不确定性感知的代理建模对于自旋体超材料的可靠性感知逆设计至关重要，未来工作将扩展至非线性响应。

Abstract: Spinodoid metamaterials offer a broad, tunable design space for anisotropic mechanical properties, yet their structure-property relationships are commonly treated as representative mappings from cone-angle descriptors to single effective stiffness values. This deterministic view overlooks the stochastic nature of Gaussian random field (GRF)-based topology generation, where identical cone-angle descriptors can produce different morphology realizations and property scatter. Here, we present an uncertainty-aware structure-property mapping framework that reinterprets cone-angle descriptors as stochastic descriptors associated with input-dependent property distributions. Using heteroscedastic Gaussian process regression (GPR), the framework infers input-dependent predictive uncertainty from sparse one-realization-per-point data without requiring empirical variance labels at every design point. The results show that stiffness scatter differs across tensor components according to each component's mechanically active directions, and that parameter sets yielding identical mean stiffness can carry different aleatoric uncertainty. Applying this uncertainty to reliability-based design optimization (RBDO), we show that a deterministic optimum is highly susceptible to constraint violation once morphology-induced variability is considered, and that a homoscedastic RBDO formulation fails to meet the prescribed reliability target - only the heteroscedastic formulation satisfies the reliability target under the heteroscedastic uncertainty evaluation. This establishes uncertainty-aware surrogate modeling as essential for reliability-aware inverse design of spinodoid metamaterials; extending the framework to nonlinear responses remains for future work.

</details>


### [315] [Toward AI-Agent-Driven Particle Transport Simulations: Implementation of AI-Assisted Workflows for PHITS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11309)
*Tatsuhiko Sato,Shintaro Hashimoto,Tatsuhiko Ogawa,Takuya Furuta,Yuho Hirata,Seiki Ohnishi,Yasuhito Sakaki,Nobuhiro Shigyo*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将AI助手和AI代理集成到PHITS（蒙特卡洛粒子输运代码）中的代码侧策略，通过构建知识库（用于RAG）和紧凑代理参考（用于AI代理），使通用AI工具能够处理输入修改、模拟执行、结果分析等复杂工作流，而无需开发专门的代码特定应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒙特卡洛粒子输运代码（如PHITS）功能强大，但使用门槛高，需要大量专业知识进行输入准备、执行和结果分析。作者希望利用现有AI工具降低这一使用门槛。

Method: 从手册、讲座材料、样本输入、工具信息和开发者整理的注意事项中构建了两套AI就绪资源：1) 用于RAG的知识库（加载到NotebookLM提供对话支持）；2) 紧凑的代理参考（结合PHITS策略和执行规则，使Codex和Claude Code能够编辑输入文件、执行计算、检查错误、分析结果、辅助源码修改和编译）。

Result: 五项演示任务（输入修改、重复模拟、参数优化、程序编译、后处理和结果解释）表明，AI代理在提供适当资源和规则的情况下能够处理复杂的PHITS工作流。实践教训包括：精确提示、人工验证、文档齐全的样本文件、明确的执行策略和命令行工具。

Conclusion: 将AI就绪资源与粒子输运代码捆绑，可以使通用AI工具无需专门的代码特定应用即可使用，这为降低复杂科学计算代码的使用门槛提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Monte Carlo particle transport codes are powerful tools, but their use requires substantial knowledge of input preparation, execution, and result analysis. In this study, we present a code-side strategy for applying existing AI assistants and AI agents to PHITS. Two complementary sets of AI-ready resources were prepared from manuals, lecture materials, sample inputs, utility information, and developer-curated cautions: a bundled knowledge base for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG)-based assistants and a compact agent reference for direct use by AI agents. The knowledge base was loaded into NotebookLM to provide conversational PHITS support, while the agent reference was combined with PHITS-specific policies and execution rules to enable Codex and Claude Code to edit input files, execute calculations, inspect errors, analyze results, and assist with source-code modification and compilation. Five demonstration tasks covered input modification, repeated simulations, parameter optimization, program compilation, post-processing, and result interpretation. The results showed that AI agents could handle complex PHITS workflows when appropriate resources and rules were provided. Practical lessons included precise prompts, human verification, well-documented sample files, explicit execution policies, and command-line-accessible tools. These findings support bundling AI-ready resources with particle transport codes to enable the use of general-purpose AI tools without requiring dedicated code-specific applications.

</details>
